docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-07-12-codex-round3-of-rev3.md:16:2. **Energy quanta are non-commensurate (STILL-BROKEN; #5, #15, B1).** With mass quantum `1e-9` and energy quantum `1e-3` and energy density `e_N`, one mass quantum ≈ **3.581** energy quanta — not an integer — so structural chemical energy cannot enter an *exact* int64 energy equality. Exact-integer conservation works cleanly for a single currency but breaks at the mass↔energy conversion.
docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-07-12-codex-round3-of-rev3.md:17:3. **Reserve economy has no inflow (STILL-BROKEN; #15).** Rev-3 removed the grazing reserve-credit to stop the double-mint but supplied no replacement partition, so reserve only decreases — no valid feeding path.

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:214:### D2.5 — Kinetic energy is the f32 mechanical arm, never an int64 reservoir (closes NP-8)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:216:Two arms, one canonical representation of KE. D2.5 fixes **which identity gate (b) uses** and **what may
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:217:enter the int64 energy ledger**; it does **not** redefine `p_in` — the authoritative input-power identity
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:228:  channel, used identically in the S0 gate, the ecology ledger, and the diagnostic (§6.1's single-symbol
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:232:  int64 reservoir.** Storing `E_KE` in int64 *and* deriving it from velocity would be two synced copies of
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:233:  one quantity, the exact anti-pattern Law 4 forbids. So `E_KE` is computed from f32 state
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:234:  (`½vᵀM v`) and checked by the **f32 mechanical arm** (gate (b) / §6.2 `R_step`), never summed into the
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:235:  int64 `Σ_energy_q`. **Delete any claim that `E_KE` (f32) enters `Σ_energy_q`** (Rev-3 §6.4 line 404).
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:237:- **The int64 energy ledger (D2.2) is a *metabolic* ledger.** It conserves chemical/thermal energy across
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:238:  `{reserve, E_sun_cum, E_heat_cum, E_export_cum}`. Muscle mechanical output is booked as an **exact
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:239:  `reserve → E_heat_cum` debit at the metabolic timescale** — the full spend `(P_basal + p_in/η)·dt`
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:240:  (D2.3), i.e. locomotion chemical energy is accounted *dissipated*. This closes exactly per-step in int64
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:241:  (reserve loses `n_e` quanta, `E_heat_cum` gains the same `n_e`).
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:244:  mechanical exchange (muscle work → KE → wake/drag heat within a stroke) lives entirely in the f32 arm;
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:245:  its net over a gait cycle is zero (`∮ ΔKE = 0`), so the metabolic "all-spend-to-heat" booking is exact at
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:247:  §6.2 `R_step`, `τ = 1e-6/step`, drift `1e-4`, bounded-oscillating) — **never** an int64 leak, because it
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:248:  is never an int64 ledger member in the first place.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md:250:- **Delete §6.3 line-394's ledger reading** of `ΔE_KE = v_mid·F_stream·Dt + ½vᵀΔMv`: that identity is

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:1:# SirRobin — Implementation Plan Rev. 3: Reconciliation & Final Fixes
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:5:This document reconciles the **six independently-produced Rev-2 fix-sets into one internally-consistent spec** and closes every residual gap Codex named. It does **not** re-open any decision Rev-2 got right; where Rev-2 fix-sets disagreed, Rev-3 states the single canonical decision, specifies it concretely (equations with units, tensor shapes, pseudocode, and the gate that enforces it), and lists exactly which Codex findings and new-problems it closes. **Rev-2 + Rev-3 together are the final S0 spec.** Where Rev-3 supersedes a Rev-2 mechanism (e.g. the f64/TwoSum ledger), the Rev-2 text is deleted, not layered over.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:7:Physics ground truth is cited by `SwimEval.cs:line`. The nine non-negotiable laws (fidelity, conservation, form-is-function, single-canonical-representation, clean-boundaries, continuity, grow-on-demand, depth-first, implicit-selection) are the acceptance frame; every reconciliation below is chosen to *strengthen* one of them, and this is called out.

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:73:## 1. Conservation ledger — int64 fixed-point quanta (supersedes Rev-2 §5.1–5.3)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:77:**Root decision.** The wrong-sign TwoSum is not patched — it is **deleted**. Conserved *reservoir state* is stored as **int64 fixed-point quanta**, not f64. Integer transfers are exact by construction: there is no rounding, therefore no residual, therefore no sign to get wrong. Physics and rates stay f32 (hot loop) / f64 (Lamb quadrature, oracle arm) exactly as #11 specifies; **only the conserved reservoir totals are int64.** This makes `close_books()` an *exact integer equality* rather than a tolerance, and makes the reduction of totals order-independent (integer addition is associative and exact — deterministic on any device regardless of reduction tree), which is what B1's "deterministic reduction of the error terms" asks for: there are no error terms.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:79:### 1.1 Currencies and quanta (frozen)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:84:| energy | J | `q_energy = 1e-3` J (milli-J) | `9.2e15` J/world | reserve/heat/sun meters; 1 mol biomass = `e_N=3.581e6` J = `3.581e9` quanta |
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:88:### 1.2 Reservoir tensors (replaces the f64 rows of Rev-2 §5.1)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:93:| meters: `E_sun_cum, E_heat_cum, E_export_cum` | `[W]` | **int64** | energy |
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:96:**Biomass mass/energy reservoir totals (`Bp`, `E_chem`, and the biomass part of any mass sum) are NOT stored here** — they are *derived masked sums* over ColonyState per resolution 5. `energy` (reserve) and `struct_N` live in ColonyState as int64 (resolution 5), and are summed into the ledger as derived totals. The int64 dtype for `energy`/`struct_N` **replaces** the f64 promotion Rev-2 §4.3 gave them (that f64 choice is deleted; NP-9's "every field reservoir is f64" contradiction dissolves — reservoirs are int64, an 8-byte whole-tick cost G-E2E measures, not an "O(W) f64 island").
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:98:### 1.3 Exact integer transfer (replaces Rev-2 §5.2 `two_sum`/`transfer`)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:105:No quantum is minted or destroyed: the identical integer `moved` is subtracted from the source and added to the destination. `two_sum`, `R_num`, and the `-=`/`+=` sign question are **removed from the codebase** (Rev-2 §5.2 deleted).
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:107:### 1.4 Quantizing a real flux with a deterministic remainder carry
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:117:# usage: n,carry = commit_flux(rate*dt, q_mass, carry_SD, mask); src_q,dst_q = transfer_quanta(src_q,dst_q,n,mask)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:122:### 1.5 close_books — exact integer equality (replaces Rev-2 §5.3 tolerances for the *bookkeeping* arm)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:132:`Σ(all reservoir quanta) == I0_quanta + Σ(external-source quanta) − Σ(external-sink quanta)`, as an **`int64` equality with zero tolerance.** For a closed box (S1 mass, no external in/out) this is `Σ quanta == I0_quanta` exactly. The physics/work-consistency arm (E_KE from the f32 kernel, `p_in`, `wDrag`) remains a *tolerance* gate at `τ_E_phys = 1e-6` per step / `1e-4` drift, bounded-oscillating — that arm is a **fidelity** check on the f32 kernel, never a conservation leak (resolution 6). The two arms are now cleanly separated: bookkeeping is exact-integer, physics-consistency is f32-tolerance.

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:157:- **Parent convention (single):** `seg_parent[b,k] ∈ [0, S_slot)` is a **creature-local** slot index. A **root's parent = slot 0** (the sentinel), **never −1, never self.** Self-parent (Rev-2 §4.2) is deleted; global `body_base+local` is deleted; `−1` (Rev-2 §9 root sentinel value) is deleted. Every parent gather `pos[b, seg_parent[b,k]]` is in-bounds and, for a root, returns the identity sentinel → `pPos=0, pRot=identity`, bit-matching `SwimEval.cs:669` (`parentIndex<0 → pPos=0, pRot=identity`).

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:204:# 2x2 constrained system (resolution 7): M = [[M00,M02],[M02,M22]], P=(Px,Pz)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:216:For an empty body `M00=M02=M22=0` and `P=0` (no segments ⇒ `F_stream=0`): `det=0 → det_safe=1 → dv=0/1=0`, then masked to 0. **Output is finite (zero accel).** For a *valid but near-singular* body (slender/anisotropic), `degenerate` is triggered only by the scale-relative `det` test, and the regularized path of resolution 7 handles it *with the reaction impulse ledgered* — it is **not** silently floored here. The absolute `1e-12` floor (`SwimEval.cs:1157`) is retained **only** inside `solve_sym3_donor` for gain0 byte-conformance (resolution 7), never in production.

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:224:**Closes:** Codex **#13** (RNG stream stability), Codex **#14** (allocator disagreement: §13 used per-world `next_eid[W]`, §4/§14 used a global host `next_stable_id`), blocker **B6** (one allocator + world-unique keys), and new-problems **NP-4** (per-world IDs without a world key collide), **NP-5** (the false "32×32 product fits in int64 exactly" claim), **NP-6** (Box–Muller `log(0)`, rejection exhaustion undefined). Strengthens Law 4 (single canonical representation) and the determinism contract.

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:298:All intermediates `< 2^33 ≪ 2^63`; no overflow. (The false-arithmetic claim is the exact kind of unverified assertion this gate exists to catch.)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:300:### 4.5 Distribution transforms + exhaustion rule (closes NP-6)
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:303:- **Box–Muller (no `log(0)`):** use `u1 ∈ (0,1]` by offsetting to exclude 0: `u1 = ((w0>>8) + 1) * 2**-24 ∈ [2^-24, 1]`; `u2 = (w1>>8)*2**-24 ∈ [0,1)`. Then `r = sqrt(-2*ln(u1))` (finite: `ln(u1)≤0`, `=0` only at `u1=1` giving `r=0`), `z0=r·cos(2π u2)`, `z1=r·sin(2π u2)`. `u1` can never be 0 ⇒ `ln(0)` is unreachable.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:305:- **Rejection sampling exhaustion (defined):** a rejection sampler consumes `draw_index = 0..255`. If all 256 sub-draws are exhausted (probability `< 2^-256` for any well-formed sampler), it **re-keys**: `event_kind |= 0x80` (the reserved `REKEY_CONT` bit), `draw_index` resets to 0, and an `attempt` counter is folded into the freed high bits. This yields effectively unbounded draws while remaining a pure deterministic function of the logical address + attempt number. In practice S0–S3 samplers (Gaussian, Bernoulli, categorical-by-inversion) never reject, so `REKEY_CONT` is defense-in-depth.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:307:### 4.6 Tests (extend Rev-2 §13.6)

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:375:constrained update:  M_{n+1}(v_{n+1} - v_n) = P + J_c + J_reg,   P = F_stream·Dt,  v_y ≡ 0

It does so by (a) **correcting the regularization sign** — the near-singular solve `(M+rI)Δv=P` implies `MΔv=P−r·Δv`, so the booked impulse is `J_reg = −r·Δv`, not the `+r·Δv` Rev-3 §7.3 wrote; (b) **adopting a two-currency conservation architecture** in which mass (mass-quanta) and energy (energy-quanta) each close in their own int64 ledger and are *never* summed into one total nor converted quantum-to-quantum inside a conservation equality — which **dissolves** the non-commensurate `3.581 energy-quanta-per-mass-quantum` problem at its root, because that number only ever arose from §6.4 summing a mass-derived chemical energy into the energy total; and (c) **closing the remaining mechanism gaps** (reserve inflow, source-availability caps, int64 overflow bound, death-transaction ordering), **RNG details** (a real continuation address with dedicated `attempt` bits, the corrected probability claim, the Box–Muller endpoint, the Tier-2 restatement), **oracle fixtures** (frozen inputs / expected / quadrature / tolerance via a standalone independent generator), and the **doc contradictions** the internal-consistency audit flagged.

    24	
    25	## D1 — Regularization sign + one solver branch
    26	
    27	**Supersedes / deletes in Rev-3.**
    28	
    29	- **§7.3 (line 457) — sign is backwards, DELETED.** Rev-3 wrote
    30	  `J_reg = [ (Mr00-M00)*dvx_reg + 0, (Mr22-M22)*dvz_reg ] = reg * dv_reg` and built *both* the momentum
    31	  gate (§7.3 line 459) and the `R_step` energy gate (§6.2 line 379) on that `+reg·dv`. Deleted.
    32	- **§7.1 (lines 427–428) — "exact governs every gate," DELETED.** Rev-3 wrote *"For every conservation
    33	  gate (INV-CONSERVE, INV-ENERGY, `R_step`, momentum), the solve is the exact unregularized system,"*
    34	  which directly contradicts §7.3 line 459 (*"Production `solve_constrained_xz` regularizes-and-ledgers"*).
    35	  Codex: "Both can't govern the same step." Deleted; replaced by the single dispatch rule below.
    36	- **§3 (lines 202–214) — dispatch bug, CORRECTED.** Rev-3 computes `degenerate` and `det_safe=1` but then
    37	  masks the result **only** on `body_valid` (`dvx = where(body_valid, dvx, 0)`). A *valid-but-degenerate*
    38	  body therefore keeps the raw `numerator/det_safe = numerator/1` result and **`Δv_reg` is never selected**.
    39	  Corrected to an explicit three-way select.
    40	
    41	**The algebra (the whole bug).** The near-singular solve is `(M+rI)Δv = P`, hence `M Δv = P − r·Δv`. The
    42	booked regularization impulse is therefore
    43	
    44	    J_reg = −r·Δv          [units: kg·m/s]        (NOT +r·Δv)
    45	
    46	Momentum then closes **exactly**: `M Δv = P + J_c + J_reg`, since `P + (−r·Δv) = P − r·Δv = M Δv` and
    47	`J_c` (vertical-plane reaction, `SwimEval.cs:807`) is workless (`v_y ≡ 0`). ✔
    48	
    49	**One production solver, three per-body branches, single `select` dispatch.** `M` is the symmetric 2×2
    50	constrained added-mass matrix `[[M00,M02],[M02,M22]]`; `P = F_stream·Dt` is `[B,2]`; all tensors are
    51	`[B]`/`[B,2]` with `B = W·N_cap`.
    52	
    53	```python
    54	# branch predicates ([B] bool) — mutually exclusive, exhaustive
    55	body_valid  = alive & (masked_segment_sum(seg_mass, seg_mask) > 0)     # §3 (unchanged)
    56	degenerate  = det(M) < det_floor        # equivalently λ_min < λ_floor, i.e. κ > KAPPA_MAX (§7.2)
    57	INVALID     = ~body_valid                                              # empty / zero-mass
    58	REGULARIZED = body_valid & degenerate                                  # valid but near-singular
    59	EXACT       = body_valid & ~degenerate                                 # the common path
    60	
    61	# per-branch solves
    62	Δv_exact = solve_2x2(M00, M02, M22, Px, Pz)                            # exact KKT
    63	Δv_reg   = solve_2x2(M00+reg, M02, M22+reg, Px, Pz)                    # reg = EPS_SPD·tr  (§7.3 form kept)
    64	
    65	# THE dispatch — Δv_reg is actually selected on the REGULARIZED branch (fixes §3)
    66	Δv    = where(INVALID, 0.0,
    67	          where(REGULARIZED, Δv_reg, Δv_exact))                        # [B,2]
    68	J_reg = where(REGULARIZED, -reg * Δv_reg, 0.0)                         # [B,2]  ← the − sign
    69	```
    70	
    71	- `degenerate := det(M) < det_floor` (or `λ_min < λ_floor`) is computed with the **cancellation-free**
    72	  `disc = sqrt((M00−M22)² + 4·M02²)`, `λ_max = ½(tr+disc)`, `λ_min = det/λ_max` form Rev-3 §7.2 already
    73	  fixed — **kept verbatim**. `tr = M00+M22 > 0` for any valid SPD body; `tr=0` occurs only for an
    74	  `INVALID` body, which this dispatch forces to `Δv=0` *before* any division (Rev-3 §7.2's masking claim
    75	  now holds because the branch, not a post-hoc `body_valid` mask, governs selection).
    76	- **The `INVALID` branch contributes nothing to any gate** (`Δv=0`, `J_reg=0`). For an empty body
    77	  `M=0, P=0` ⇒ `Δv_exact=0/det_safe`; the `INVALID` select overrides it to exactly `0` — finite, no
    78	  `1/0`, no `0·∞` (Rev-3 §3's intent, now actually wired).
    79	- **REGULARIZED's work is accounted in the f32 `R_step` arm** per D2.6 (the `v_mid·J_reg` term, gate (b)) —
    80	  **no int64 booking**; on the `EXACT` branch `J_reg=0 ⇒ W_reg=0`.
    81	
    82	**Repaired `R_step` (§6.2, sign fixed).** The single KE-balance gate becomes, with `J_reg=−r·Δv`:
    83	
    84	    R_step = ΔKE − v_mid·(F_stream·Dt + J_reg) − ½·vₙᵀ·ΔM·vₙ            [J]
    85	           = ΔKE − v_mid·(F_stream·Dt − r·Δv) − ½·vₙᵀ·ΔM·vₙ  ≈ 0
    86	
    87	The mandatory added-mass term `½·vₙᵀ·ΔM·vₙ` (Rev-3 §6.2) and workless `J_c` are unchanged; only the sign
    88	carried into the `v_mid·J_reg` term is corrected. On the `EXACT` branch `J_reg=0` and `R_step≈0` by
    89	construction; on the `REGULARIZED` branch the `−r·Δv` term makes it close *with* the artificial damping
    90	present. Threshold unchanged: `|R_step|/max(KE_n,ε) < 1e-6` (f64) / `< 1e-3` (f32 hot), bounded-oscillating.
    91	
    92	**Kill the §7.1-vs-§7.3 contradiction (one rule governs the step).** Production is the per-body branch
    93	above — **not** "the exact unregularized system for every gate." The conservation gates (INV-CONSERVE,
    94	momentum, `R_step`) are evaluated on the **actually booked** impulses `P + J_c + J_reg`, so they hold on
    95	**both** the `EXACT` branch (`J_reg=0`) and the `REGULARIZED` branch (`J_reg=−r·Δv`). There is exactly one
    96	production solve per step; there is no second "gate-only exact solve" that could disagree with it. The
    97	gain0 donor-conformance path (`solve_sym3_donor`, `SwimEval.cs:1151-1166`, absolute `1e-12` floor) never
    98	regularizes and is used only against untouched-donor fixtures (Rev-3 §7.3 / §8.2, unchanged).
    99	
   100	**Gate.** `test_reg_momentum_closes` (force `κ>KAPPA_MAX` on a 10:1 slender body; assert
   101	`M·Δv == P + J_c + J_reg` to f32 rel `<1e-5`, and that flipping the sign to `+r·Δv` **fails** it — proving
   102	the sign is load-bearing); `test_reg_energy_in_rstep` (assert the f32 `R_step` closes with the `v_mid·J_reg`
   103	term included and **fails if it is dropped** — no int64 booking involved); `test_solve_dispatch_selects_reg`
   104	(**new**: a valid-degenerate body's output equals
   105	`Δv_reg`, *not* `numerator/1` — the §3 select is exercised); `test_reg_off_on_wellcond`
   106	(isotropic/H0: `reg=0`, production == exact solve, `max_abs(Δ)==0`).
   107	
   108	**Closes:** #2, #12, NP-3, B4; internal-consistency contradictions §7.1/§7.3 and §3-degenerate-dispatch.
   109	
   110	---
   111	
   112	## D2 — Two-currency energy/mass conservation architecture
   113	
   114	**Root decision — two independently-conserved currencies, never summed into one total.** Mass (mol N,
   115	`q_mass = 1e-9`) and energy (J, `q_energy = 1e-3`) each close in **their own int64 quanta**. There is **no
   116	mass→energy quantum conversion inside any conservation equality**, so the non-commensurate
   117	`e_N/q_energy·q_mass = 3.581e6 · 1e-9 / 1e-3 = 3.581` energy-quanta-per-mass-quantum problem *cannot arise*
   118	— it only ever arose because §6.4 summed a mass-derived chemical energy into the energy total. This
   119	strengthens Law 2 (the books close) and Law 4 (single canonical representation: biomass has exactly **one**
   120	representation — mass).
   121	
   122	**Supersedes / deletes in Rev-3.**
   123	
   124	- **§6.4 (line 404) — `Σ_stored` energy graph, DELETED.** Rev-3 wrote
   125	  `Σ_stored = E_chem(Bp+Bd+Bm derived) + E_chem(struct_N derived) + E_reserve + E_KE`. This sums a
   126	  mass-derived chemical energy (`e_N·struct_N`, a **float**) **and** `E_KE` (an f32 kernel quantity) into
   127	  the energy total — the exact operation that (i) makes energy non-commensurate with mass and (ii) puts
   128	  non-integer floats inside an "exact int64" equality. Deleted. Replaced by the two independent closures
   129	  D2.2 (energy) and D2.1 (mass).
   130	- **§6.4 (lines 410–411) — `INV-ENERGY (bookkeeping, EXACT int64)` over `Σ_stored_q`, DELETED** and
   131	  replaced by D2.2's `Σ_energy_q` closure over native energy reservoirs only.
   132	- **§1.1 (line 84) note "1 mol biomass = e_N=3.581e6 J = 3.581e9 quanta," DELETED** as a *conserved* claim
   133	  — `e_N·struct_N` is a diagnostic readout (D2.1), not a booked energy reservoir.
   134	- **§1.2 / §5 — the `E_chem` row in the energy reservoir table and the snapshot energy schema, DELETED**
   135	  (D2.1).
   136	- **§6.3 (line 394) `ΔE_KE = v_mid·F_stream·Dt + ½vᵀΔMv` "partition," DELETED** (D2.5): gate (b) is a
   137	  force-law power-balance identity, not a `ΔKE` partition.
   138	- **§7.3 (line 459) / §6.2 (line 379) "numerical-residual energy ledger," DELETED** (D2.6): regularization
   139	  work is accounted in the f32 `R_step` arm (the `v_mid·J_reg` term); no int64 sink, no `R_num` resurrected.
   140	
   141	### D2.1 — Structural biomass is mass, full stop
   142	
   143	`struct_N` (per-creature, `[W,N_cap]`, ColonyState) is conserved in **mass quanta only** (int64,
   144	`q_mass=1e-9` mol). Its chemical energy `E_chem = e_N·struct_N` (`e_N=3.581e6` J/mol) is a **derived float
   145	diagnostic readout [J]** — NEVER a conserved energy reservoir, NEVER summed into `Σ_energy_q`, NEVER
   146	assigned to a stored pool. `E_chem` is deleted from the energy reservoir table (Rev-3 §1.2), from the
   147	snapshot energy schema (Rev-3 §5), and from `Σ_stored` (Rev-3 §6.4); it survives only as an observe-layer
   148	readout `E_chem_creature = e_N·(struct_N_q · q_mass)`. This is the whole fix for #15's mint and #5/B1's
   149	commensurability: **no float `e_N·struct_N` ever enters an int64 energy equality**, so there is no
   150	`3.581`-quanta rounding to leak.
   151	
   152	### D2.2 — Energy currency = reserve + source + sinks, all native energy quanta
   153	
   154	Conserved **energy** reservoirs (all int64 energy-quanta, `q_energy=1e-3` J):
   155	
   156	| reservoir | shape | dtype | role |
   157	|---|---|---|---|
   158	| `reserve` | `[W,N_cap]` (ColonyState) | int64 | per-creature metabolic reserve |
   159	| `E_sun_cum` | `[W]` | int64 | environmental **source** meter (autotrophic fixation input) |
   160	| `E_heat_cum` | `[W]` | int64 | dissipation **sink** (basal + muscle inefficiency + drag/wake heat) |
   161	| `E_export_cum` | `[W]` | int64 | advective / other export **sink** |
   162	
   163	Closure (the §1.5 `close_books` identity specialized to the energy currency, with `E_sun` declared
   164	external via `declare_external`):
   165	
   166	    Σ_energy_q  :=  Σ reserve  +  E_heat_cum  +  E_export_cum  −  E_sun_cum
   167	    close_books(energy):  Σ_energy_q == I0_energy_q         # exact int64 ==, per world, every step
   168	
   169	`I0_energy_q` is the **total initial reserve captured at `reset()`** (energy quanta) — the retained §1.5
   170	general form `total == I0_q[currency] + ext`, specialized to the energy currency. It is **not** forced to
   171	`0`: initial reserves are real seeded energy, not an external injection, so conservation checks that
   172	current *stored + sinks − sources* equals what was present at `t=0`. (`declare_external(E_sun)` accounts
   173	inflow *after* `t=0`; it does not re-seed the initial state.) Rev-3 §1's `transfer_quanta` (§1.3) and
   174	`commit_flux` sub-quantum carry (§1.4) are **kept** — they are already exact *within a single currency*;
   175	**every** energy transaction uses them. `E_KE` (f32) and `E_chem` (float) are **not** members of this sum
   176	(D2.1, D2.5).
   177	
   178	### D2.3 — Reserve inflow (fixes "reserve only drains")
   179	
   180	Reserve gains energy from exactly two paths, both **exact energy-quanta** transfers; loss is one path:
   181	
   182	- **Producers (autotrophic fixation):** `E_sun_cum → reserve`, rate set by the producer's captured
   183	  environmental power `P_cap` [W]; a paired `declare_external(E_sun)` / `commit_flux(P_cap·dt, q_energy, …)`
   184	  / `transfer_quanta`. This is the previously-missing inflow.
   185	- **Consumers (predation/grazing):** two *separate* exact transactions in two *separate* currencies —
   186	  prey `reserve → predator reserve` (**energy** ledger) **AND** prey `struct_N → predator struct_N` (or
   187	  `→ Bd` detritus) (**mass** ledger). No mass↔energy conversion; structural energy is counted once (as
   188	  mass, via D2.1's `E_chem` readout).
   189	- **Loss:** the total metabolic spend `(P_basal + p_in/η)·dt` drains `reserve → E_heat_cum` (**energy**
   190	  ledger, via `commit_flux`) — basal `P_basal = B0·M^α` plus the chemical cost `p_in/η` of producing
   191	  mechanical output `p_in` at muscle efficiency `η`. At the metabolic timescale the entire spend is booked
   192	  as dissipated (see D2.5: muscle mechanical output is accounted spent-to-environment; the transient
   193	  kinetic term lives in the f32 arm, not this ledger).
   194	
   195	### D2.4 — Building structure couples the two ledgers WITHOUT converting units
   196	
   197	Growth of `Δstruct_N` mol (>0) fires **two paired transactions together**, one per currency:
   198	
   199	```python
   200	# ENERGY ledger: reserve → E_heat_cum  of magnitude  e_build · Δstruct_N   [J]
   201	n_e, carry_reserve_heat = commit_flux(e_build * Δstruct_N, q_energy, carry_reserve_heat, grow_mask)
   202	reserve_q, E_heat_cum_q = transfer_quanta(reserve_q, E_heat_cum_q, n_e, grow_mask)   # exact, energy-quanta
   203	
   204	# MASS ledger: Nd → struct_N  of magnitude  Δstruct_N   [mol]
   205	n_m, carry_Nd_struct    = commit_flux(Δstruct_N, q_mass, carry_Nd_struct, grow_mask)
   206	Nd_q, struct_N_q        = transfer_quanta(Nd_q, struct_N_q, n_m, grow_mask)           # exact, mass-quanta
   207	```
   208	
   209	`e_build` (f64, J per mol) sets the **energy-side magnitude only**; it never appears in a cross-currency
   210	equality (it multiplies a mol amount to yield a J amount that is then quantized in *energy* quanta). Both
   211	ledgers close **independently**: energy loses `n_e` quanta from `reserve` to `E_heat_cum`; mass moves `n_m`
   212	quanta from `Nd` to `struct_N`. There is no equation asserting `n_e` and `n_m` are related in quanta.
   213	
   214	### D2.5 — Kinetic energy is the f32 mechanical arm, never an int64 reservoir (closes NP-8)
   215	
   216	Two arms, one canonical representation of KE. D2.5 fixes **which identity gate (b) uses** and **what may
   217	enter the int64 energy ledger**; it does **not** redefine `p_in` — the authoritative input-power identity
   218	is **Rev-3 §6.1 verbatim** (the `tFin·U_cl` form Codex round-3 finding #1 explicitly accepted).
   219	
   220	- **Gate (b) is the §6.1 force-law power balance** (f32/f64), **not** a `ΔKE` equation:
   221	
   222	      U_cl = max(0, U)
   223	      p_in = ( tReact·U + pWake ) + ( tFin·U_cl + pFin )                    # Rev-3 §6.1 line 365, unchanged
   224	      gate (b):  | p_in − [ (tReact·U + pWake) + (tFin·U_cl + pFin) ] | / max(p_in, ε)  <  1e-6   (f32/f64)
   225	
   226	  The abbreviated `tReact·U_cl + pWake + pFin` form in the decision spec is **superseded** by §6.1's
   227	  canonical RHS: one `p_in` symbol, signed `U` on the reactive channel and clamped `U_cl` on the fin
   228	  channel, used identically in the S0 gate, the ecology ledger, and the diagnostic (§6.1's single-symbol
   229	  mandate). This is the one place the locked spec was abbreviated; §6.1 governs.

   214	### D2.5 — Kinetic energy is the f32 mechanical arm, never an int64 reservoir (closes NP-8)
   215	
   216	Two arms, one canonical representation of KE. D2.5 fixes **which identity gate (b) uses** and **what may
   217	enter the int64 energy ledger**; it does **not** redefine `p_in` — the authoritative input-power identity
   218	is **Rev-3 §6.1 verbatim** (the `tFin·U_cl` form Codex round-3 finding #1 explicitly accepted).
   219	
   220	- **Gate (b) is the §6.1 force-law power balance** (f32/f64), **not** a `ΔKE` equation:
   221	
   222	      U_cl = max(0, U)
   223	      p_in = ( tReact·U + pWake ) + ( tFin·U_cl + pFin )                    # Rev-3 §6.1 line 365, unchanged
   224	      gate (b):  | p_in − [ (tReact·U + pWake) + (tFin·U_cl + pFin) ] | / max(p_in, ε)  <  1e-6   (f32/f64)
   225	
   226	  The abbreviated `tReact·U_cl + pWake + pFin` form in the decision spec is **superseded** by §6.1's
   227	  canonical RHS: one `p_in` symbol, signed `U` on the reactive channel and clamped `U_cl` on the fin
   228	  channel, used identically in the S0 gate, the ecology ledger, and the diagnostic (§6.1's single-symbol
   229	  mandate). This is the one place the locked spec was abbreviated; §6.1 governs.
   230	
   231	- **Kinetic energy has a single canonical representation — the f32 body velocity (Law 4) — and is NOT an
   232	  int64 reservoir.** Storing `E_KE` in int64 *and* deriving it from velocity would be two synced copies of
   233	  one quantity, the exact anti-pattern Law 4 forbids. So `E_KE` is computed from f32 state
   234	  (`½vᵀM v`) and checked by the **f32 mechanical arm** (gate (b) / §6.2 `R_step`), never summed into the
   235	  int64 `Σ_energy_q`. **Delete any claim that `E_KE` (f32) enters `Σ_energy_q`** (Rev-3 §6.4 line 404).
   236	
   237	- **The int64 energy ledger (D2.2) is a *metabolic* ledger.** It conserves chemical/thermal energy across
   238	  `{reserve, E_sun_cum, E_heat_cum, E_export_cum}`. Muscle mechanical output is booked as an **exact
   239	  `reserve → E_heat_cum` debit at the metabolic timescale** — the full spend `(P_basal + p_in/η)·dt`
   240	  (D2.3), i.e. locomotion chemical energy is accounted *dissipated*. This closes exactly per-step in int64
   241	  (reserve loses `n_e` quanta, `E_heat_cum` gains the same `n_e`).
   242	
   243	- **The two arms are consistent at gait-cycle scale, not forced to reconcile per-step.** The transient
   244	  mechanical exchange (muscle work → KE → wake/drag heat within a stroke) lives entirely in the f32 arm;
   245	  its net over a gait cycle is zero (`∮ ΔKE = 0`), so the metabolic "all-spend-to-heat" booking is exact at
   246	  cycle scale. The per-step mechanical `ΔKE` is the f32 arm's **bounded-oscillating** term (gate (b) /
   247	  §6.2 `R_step`, `τ = 1e-6/step`, drift `1e-4`, bounded-oscillating) — **never** an int64 leak, because it
   248	  is never an int64 ledger member in the first place.
   249	
   250	- **Delete §6.3 line-394's ledger reading** of `ΔE_KE = v_mid·F_stream·Dt + ½vᵀΔMv`: that identity is
   251	  retained *only* as the f32 physics-consistency arm (`R_step`), not as a term added into `Σ_energy_q`. The
   252	  COM-work vs tail-throughflow distinction is carried by §6.1/§6.2's `U_cl` and the explicit `(pWake+pFin)`
   253	  wake term (§6.3 line 398), not by folding `ΔKE` into the int64 books.
   254	
   255	**Scope note (not a defer — a phase fact):** S0/SpikeSwim exercises **only gate (b)** — the f32 mechanical
   256	arm. The int64 metabolic energy ledger (`reserve`, `E_heat_cum`, …) first *exists* at S1 (Kleiber read,
   257	Rev-3 §9/plan line 524) and closes end-to-end at S3 (`test_energy_loop_closes_e2e`). D2.5 fixes the
   258	architecture now so no float ever enters the int64 books; the S3 gate proves the metabolic ledger closes
   259	against a live economy.
   260	
   261	### D2.6 — Regularization work is accounted in the f32 mechanical arm, not the int64 ledger
   262	
   263	Rev-3 §1.3 (line 105) deletes `R_num`, but §§6–7 (§6.2 line 379, §7.3 line 459) then book regularization
   264	work into a "numerical-residual energy ledger" that no longer exists in the table or schema. **Fix
   265	(consistent with D2.5):** regularization modifies the **f32 mechanical solve**, so its work is accounted
   266	**entirely in the f32 §6.2 `R_step` balance** — the `v_mid·J_reg` term — and is **NOT booked into any int64
   267	reservoir.** The "numerical-residual energy ledger" is **deleted outright**, not redirected: there is no
   268	int64 sink for it, because regularization never touches the metabolic ledger. This is the D2.5-consistent
   269	resolution of the §1.3-vs-§§6-7 contradiction (an int64 `reserve → E_heat_cum` booking would have been a
   270	second, int64 copy of an f32 mechanical quantity — the Law-4 anti-pattern D2.5 forbids).
   271	
   272	- **Why no int64 booking.** The int64 energy ledger (D2.5) is metabolic; it sees only the muscle spend
   273	  `(P_basal + p_in/η)·dt`, which is set by muscle activation and is **unchanged** by whether the 2×2 solve
   274	  was regularized. Regularization alters only the body's f32 velocity response (an f32 KE detail), so its
   275	  energy lives in the f32 arm.
   276	- **Where it closes.** On the `REGULARIZED` branch, gate (b) / §6.2 `R_step` closes *because* it carries the
   277	  `v_mid·J_reg` term:
   278	
   279	      R_step = ΔKE − v_mid·(F_stream·Dt + J_reg) − ½·vₙᵀ·ΔM·vₙ  ≈ 0        (J_reg = −r·Δv)
   280	
   281	  On the `EXACT` branch `J_reg = 0`, the term vanishes and `R_step ≈ 0` identically. Threshold unchanged
   282	  (`|R_step|/max(KE_n, ε) < 1e-6` f64 / `1e-3` f32, bounded-oscillating).
   283	
   284	**Work term — midpoint form (locked).** `W_reg = v_mid·J_reg` with `v_mid = ½(vₙ + vₙ₊₁)` — work is impulse
   285	× mean velocity, the physically correct energy and the form §6.2's `R_step` uses (the decision spec's
   286	literal `J_reg·Δv` is superseded). It appears in exactly one place — the f32 `R_step` term — so there is no
   287	second copy and no int64 mirror to drift.
   288	
   289	### D2.7 — Source-availability cap (a reservoir may never go negative)
   290	
   291	`transfer_quanta` gains a hard precondition — it may never move more than the source holds:
   292	
   293	```python
   294	def transfer_quanta(src_q, dst_q, n, mask):          # all int64; n>=0
   295	    n_eff = torch.minimum(torch.where(mask, n, 0), src_q)   # cap at availability; never overdraw
   296	    return src_q - n_eff, dst_q + n_eff, (torch.where(mask, n, 0) - n_eff)  # shortfall returned
   297	```
   298	
   299	The shortfall `n − n_eff > 0` triggers the caller's defined failure path: **maintenance/locomotion that
   300	cannot be paid from `reserve` marks the creature for starvation death this tick** (not a negative reserve).
   301	A debug-gated assert `(src_q >= 0).all()` runs in `close_books`. This closes the new "transfer permits
   302	`n > src` → negative reservoirs while 'conserving' the total" hazard.
   303	
   304	### D2.8 — Death-transaction ordering (fixed, gated)
   305	
   306	Fixed lifecycle order — egress precedes flag-clear so no mass/energy vanishes from `close_books`:
   307	
   308	1. **Compute `die_mask`** (`[W,N_cap]` bool).
   309	2. **While dying creatures are still ledger-live**, egress their **mass** (`struct_N → Bd`, exact
   310	   mass-quanta) and **energy** (`reserve → E_heat_cum` / `E_sun_cum`-return, exact energy-quanta) via
   311	   `transfer_quanta`.
   312	3. **Only then** clear `alive_mask` and free the slots.
   313	
   314	**Gate.** `test_cohort_death_conserves` (**new**): kill a whole cohort in one tick and assert **both**
   315	ledgers `close_books() == 0` (exact) across the death step — catching the ordering hazard where clearing
   316	`alive` before the `struct_N → Bd` transfer would drop mass from the sum.
   317	
   318	### D2.9 — Overflow bound (checked, not asserted)
   319	
   320	World-lifetime headroom: with `q_energy=1e-3`, signed int64 holds `2^63−1 ≈ 9.22e18` quanta ⇒ `9.2e15` J
   321	per world. `close_books` asserts **no reservoir and no cumulative meter exceeds `2^62`** (leaves a 2×
   322	headroom margin) for both currencies; the derived bound
   323	
   324	    W · N_cap · reserve_max  +  E_sun_cum(t_max)   <   2^62          # energy
   325	    W · N_cap · struct_N_max  +  Σ_abiotic_mass     <   2^62          # mass
   326	
   327	is pinned in `config.py` and re-checked at every snapshot. Same construction for mass with `q_mass=1e-9`
   328	(`9.2e9` mol/world). This closes the new "no overflow proof" gap.
   329	
   330	**Master balance (open system) — two independent exact-integer closures replacing Rev-3 §6.4's
   331	`INV-ENERGY`:**
   332	
   333	    INV-MASS   (EXACT int64):  Σ struct_N + Σ Bp + Σ Nd + Σ Bd + Σ Sed == I0_mass_q
   334	                               # ALL mass reservoirs (Rev-3 §1.2): struct_N (ColonyState) + Bp (derived
   335	                               # producer-biomass masked sum) + Nd, Bd, Sed (abiotic). No float member.
   336	    INV-ENERGY (EXACT int64):  Σ reserve + E_heat_cum + E_export_cum − E_sun_cum == I0_energy_q
   337	                               # metabolic reservoirs only (D2.5). E_KE, E_chem are NOT members.
   338	    (physics-consistency arm, f32 tolerance, unchanged §1.5): the f32 E_KE tracks §6.2's R_step to
   339	       τ_E_phys=1e-6/step, 1e-4 drift, bounded-oscillating — a fidelity check, never a conservation leak.
   340	
   341	**Gates.** `test_reserve_inflow_partitions` (grazing/fixation cycle: reserve strictly can *increase*; no
   342	mint; `Σ_energy_q` and `Σ_mass_q` both exact-close); `test_struct_energy_counted_once` (grazing→death
   343	cycle: `E_chem` never assigned to a stored pool; both ledgers exact-close); `test_cohort_death_conserves`
   344	(D2.8); `test_transfer_no_negative` (D2.7 cap; assert `(src_q>=0).all()`); `test_overflow_bound`
   345	(D2.9 `<2^62` assertion fires on a synthetic over-cap world); `test_energy_no_float_in_ledger` (AST:
   346	neither `E_KE` nor `e_N·struct_N` is ever added into `Σ_energy_q`).
   347

   354	## D3 — RNG rejection-continuation + Box–Muller + Tier-2 statement
   355	
   356	**Supersedes / deletes in Rev-3.**
   357	
   358	- **§4.5 (line 305) — "an `attempt` counter is folded into the freed high bits," DELETED.** Rev-3's 7-field
   359	  address (§4.2) already uses all 128 counter bits (`16+36+40+20+8+8 = 128`); there are **no** freed high
   360	  bits, so the rejection-continuation had nowhere to put `attempt`. Re-budgeted below.
   361	- **§4.5 (line 305) — "probability `< 2^-256` for any well-formed sampler," CORRECTED** to the true
   362	  `(1−p_accept)^N` bound with a stated per-sampler `p_accept` floor.
   363	- **§4.6 (line 309) — endpoint test `w0 ∈ {0, 2^24-1}`, CORRECTED** to `{0, 2^32−1}`: the raw word that
   364	  drives `u1 → 1` is `2^32−1`, not `2^24−1`.
   365	- **§0 / §4 — Tier-2 wording, RESTATED** to match Codex's accepted caveat (float Box–Muller is not itself
   366	  bit-exact under relaxed float).
   367	
   368	### D3.1 — Real continuation address (dedicated `attempt` bits)
   369	
   370	Re-budget the 128-bit Philox-4x32 counter so the rejection `attempt` index has **dedicated bits**.
   371	Canonical layout (sums to exactly 128, includes `attempt`):
   372	
   373	| field | bits | note |
   374	|---|---|---|
   375	| `world_id` | 16 | ≤65536 worlds |
   376	| `entity_id` | 32 | per-world stable id |
   377	| `purpose` | 8 | event_kind (§4.3 enum) |
   378	| `element` | 24 | gene/innovation + draw index |
   379	| `attempt` | 8 | **dedicated** rejection-continuation index → ≤256 tries |
   380	| `step_lo` | 40 | tick counter (1.1e12 ticks) |
   381	
   382	`16 + 32 + 8 + 24 + 8 + 40 = 128` — exactly the four 32-bit counter words. This **re-budgets** (supersedes)
   383	the §4.2 table, which spent all 128 bits without an `attempt` field. `attempt` gets ≥8 dedicated bits
   384	(widths of `step_lo`/`element` may be traded as long as `attempt ≥ 8`). `_assert_field_widths` raises on
   385	overflow of any field. If `attempt` exhausts (see D3.2), the sampler **clamps to a defined valid value and
   386	logs** — never undefined behaviour, never a re-key into "freed" bits that do not exist.
   387	
   388	**Field mapping to §4.2 (locked).** The renamed fields correspond to §4.2's exactly: `purpose ≡
   389	event_kind`; `element` subsumes §4.2's `gene_iid + draw_index` (one 24-bit element index over the
   390	per-purpose draw space); `entity_id ≡ stable_entity_id`; `step_lo ≡ step`. This **re-slices** §4.2's
   391	128-bit partition to carve out the 8-bit `attempt` field (taken from the previously over-wide
   392	`element`/`step` budget); the only invariants that must hold are **(i)** total = 128 bits, **(ii)** the
   393	map from `(world_id, entity_id, purpose, element, attempt, step_lo)` to counter is injective, and
   394	**(iii)** `attempt ≥ 8`. `_assert_field_widths` enforces (i) and (iii); `test_prf_reference_vectors`
   395	(regenerated for the new partition) pins (ii).
   396	
   397	### D3.2 — Correct the probability claim
   398	
   399	Rejection failure after `N` tries is exactly `(1 − p_accept)^N`, given a **stated per-sampler `p_accept`
   400	floor**. For uniform-in-range acceptance, `p_accept ≥ 0.5`, so the `attempt:8` budget (`N=256`) gives
   401	failure `≤ 0.5^256 < 2^-256`. Replace the false blanket "any well-formed sampler `< 2^-256`" with this
   402	per-sampler statement. S0–S3 samplers (Gaussian via Box–Muller, Bernoulli, categorical-by-inversion) never
   403	reject, so the continuation is defense-in-depth.
   404	
   405	### D3.3 — Box–Muller `log(0)` + corrected endpoint fixture
   406	
   407	Map the radius uniform to `u ∈ [ulp, 1]` (**exclude 0**) so `log(u)` is finite:
   408	`u1 = ((w0 >> 8) & 0xFFFFFF + 1) · 2^-24 ∈ [2^-24, 1]`, then `r = sqrt(−2·ln(u1))` (finite; `=0` only at
   409	`u1=1`), `z0 = r·cos(2π u2)`, `z1 = r·sin(2π u2)`.
   410	
   411	**Endpoint fixture (corrected).** The upper endpoint `u1 → 1` requires the top 24 bits of the raw word to
   412	be all ones, i.e. the raw word `w0 = 2^32−1` (`0xFFFFFFFF`) ⇒ `(w0>>8) & 0xFFFFFF = 0xFFFFFF` ⇒ `u1 = 1`.
   413	Rev-3 §4.6's `w0 = 2^24−1` (`0x00FFFFFF`) gives `(w0>>8) & 0xFFFFFF = 0x00FFFF` ⇒ `u1 ≈ 0.0039` — it never
   414	reaches the endpoint, so the test never exercised `r=0`.
   415	
   416	**Gate (corrected).** `test_boxmuller_no_log0`: sweep raw `w0 ∈ {0x00000000, 0xFFFFFFFF}` ⇒
   417	`u1 ∈ {2^-24, 1}`; assert `z0, z1` finite at both endpoints and `r == 0` exactly at `u1 = 1`.
   418	
   419	### D3.4 — Restate Tier-2 (determinism)

    73	## 1. Conservation ledger — int64 fixed-point quanta (supersedes Rev-2 §5.1–5.3)
    74	
    75	**Closes:** Codex **#5 STILL-BROKEN** (the f64 TwoSum residual had the wrong sign, `R_num -= (e_s+e_d)` where closure needs `+=`), blocker **B1** (fix the residual sign + specify a deterministic reduction of the error terms), and new-problem **NP-9** (f64-reservoir vs "O(W) f64 islands" inconsistency). Strengthens Law 2 (the books close) and Law 4 (single canonical representation).
    76	
    77	**Root decision.** The wrong-sign TwoSum is not patched — it is **deleted**. Conserved *reservoir state* is stored as **int64 fixed-point quanta**, not f64. Integer transfers are exact by construction: there is no rounding, therefore no residual, therefore no sign to get wrong. Physics and rates stay f32 (hot loop) / f64 (Lamb quadrature, oracle arm) exactly as #11 specifies; **only the conserved reservoir totals are int64.** This makes `close_books()` an *exact integer equality* rather than a tolerance, and makes the reduction of totals order-independent (integer addition is associative and exact — deterministic on any device regardless of reduction tree), which is what B1's "deterministic reduction of the error terms" asks for: there are no error terms.
    78	
    79	### 1.1 Currencies and quanta (frozen)
    80	
    81	| Currency | Unit | Quantum `q` | int64 capacity | Rationale |
    82	|---|---|---|---|---|
    83	| mass | mol N | `q_mass = 1e-9` mol (nano-mol) | `9.22e18·q = 9.2e9` mol/world | resolution 1e-15 relative to a ~1e6 mol world — far below f64 eps |
    84	| energy | J | `q_energy = 1e-3` J (milli-J) | `9.2e15` J/world | reserve/heat/sun meters; 1 mol biomass = `e_N=3.581e6` J = `3.581e9` quanta |
    85	
    86	Quanta are declared once in `core/config.py` (`QUANTUM = {'mass': 1e-9, 'energy': 1e-3}`) and are part of `config_hash` (determinism depends on them). A reservoir tensor's dtype is **int64**; its physical value is `quanta * q`.
    87	
    88	### 1.2 Reservoir tensors (replaces the f64 rows of Rev-2 §5.1)
    89	
    90	| Reservoir | shape | dtype | currency |
    91	|---|---|---|---|
    92	| abiotic: `Nd, Bd, Sed` | `[W,G,G,B]` / `[W,G,G]` | **int64** | mass |
    93	| meters: `E_sun_cum, E_heat_cum, E_export_cum` | `[W]` | **int64** | energy |
    94	| `carry[src,dst,currency]` (§1.4) | per directed channel `[W,...]` | **f64** | — (sub-quantum accumulator) |
    95	
    96	**Biomass mass/energy reservoir totals (`Bp`, `E_chem`, and the biomass part of any mass sum) are NOT stored here** — they are *derived masked sums* over ColonyState per resolution 5. `energy` (reserve) and `struct_N` live in ColonyState as int64 (resolution 5), and are summed into the ledger as derived totals. The int64 dtype for `energy`/`struct_N` **replaces** the f64 promotion Rev-2 §4.3 gave them (that f64 choice is deleted; NP-9's "every field reservoir is f64" contradiction dissolves — reservoirs are int64, an 8-byte whole-tick cost G-E2E measures, not an "O(W) f64 island").
    97	
    98	### 1.3 Exact integer transfer (replaces Rev-2 §5.2 `two_sum`/`transfer`)
    99	
   100	```python
   101	def transfer_quanta(src_q, dst_q, n, mask):     # all int64; n>=0 quanta to move where mask
   102	    moved = torch.where(mask, n, 0)             # elementwise, deterministic, no scatter
   103	    return src_q - moved, dst_q + moved         # EXACT: one integer leaves src, the same enters dst
   104	```
   105	No quantum is minted or destroyed: the identical integer `moved` is subtracted from the source and added to the destination. `two_sum`, `R_num`, and the `-=`/`+=` sign question are **removed from the codebase** (Rev-2 §5.2 deleted).
   106	
   107	### 1.4 Quantizing a real flux with a deterministic remainder carry
   108	
   109	A physical flux is `f = rate·dt` (f32/f64). To move it into int64 quanta without loss, each directed channel `(src→dst, currency)` keeps a **f64 carry** ∈ `[0, q)` — sub-quantum flux not yet committed. It is *owned by the source* (it has not left) and is snapshot state (so replay is exact):
   110	
   111	```python
   112	def commit_flux(f, q, carry, mask):             # f: f64 physical amount >=0; returns (n_quanta:int64, carry:f64)
   113	    acc      = torch.where(mask, f + carry, carry)          # accumulate intent in the source's currency
   114	    n        = torch.floor(acc / q).to(torch.int64)         # whole quanta to commit (deterministic floor)
   115	    carry    = acc - n.to(torch.float64) * q                # bounded in [0,q); stays in source
   116	    return n, carry
   117	# usage: n,carry = commit_flux(rate*dt, q_mass, carry_SD, mask); src_q,dst_q = transfer_quanta(src_q,dst_q,n,mask)
   118	```
   119	
   120	The carry is a rate-integrator, **not stored mass**: the reservoir's physical content is exactly `quanta·q`; the carry only decides *when* a whole quantum commits. Lumpiness is at the `1e-9` mol / `1e-3` J scale — orders below any biological relevance. Signed fluxes are split into two non-negative directed channels (src→dst and dst→src) so `f≥0` always and `floor` is unambiguous.
   121	
   122	### 1.5 close_books — exact integer equality (replaces Rev-2 §5.3 tolerances for the *bookkeeping* arm)
   123	
   124	```python
   125	def close_books(currency) -> bool:
   126	    total = int64_masked_sum(all_reservoir_quanta[currency])      # exact, order-independent
   127	    ext   = X_in_cum_q[currency] - X_out_cum_q[currency]          # metered external source/sink quanta
   128	    return total == I0_q[currency] + ext                          # EXACT ==, per world
   129	```
   130	
   131	**INV-CONSERVE (the top CI gate, exact):** for every currency and every world, at every step,
   132	`Σ(all reservoir quanta) == I0_quanta + Σ(external-source quanta) − Σ(external-sink quanta)`, as an **`int64` equality with zero tolerance.** For a closed box (S1 mass, no external in/out) this is `Σ quanta == I0_quanta` exactly. The physics/work-consistency arm (E_KE from the f32 kernel, `p_in`, `wDrag`) remains a *tolerance* gate at `τ_E_phys = 1e-6` per step / `1e-4` drift, bounded-oscillating — that arm is a **fidelity** check on the f32 kernel, never a conservation leak (resolution 6). The two arms are now cleanly separated: bookkeeping is exact-integer, physics-consistency is f32-tolerance.
   133	
   134	### 1.6 Tests (replace Rev-2 §5.3 test block)
   135	
   136	| test | asserts | threshold |
   137	|---|---|---|
   138	| `test_transfer_exact_int` | after N random `transfer_quanta`, `Σ quanta == I0_q` | exact `==` |
   139	| `test_cross_magnitude_int` | move 1 quantum between a 1-quantum pool and a `1e15`-quantum pool; both change by exactly 1 | exact `==` (the case that minted under f32/f64) |
   140	| `test_carry_bounded_and_conservative` | 1e6-step soak: every `carry ∈ [0,q)`; `Σ quanta == I0_q` throughout; carries snapshot/restore identically | exact |
   141	| `test_close_books_order_independent` | `int64_masked_sum` gives identical total under reversed/shuffled reduction order and on CPU vs CUDA | exact `==` |
   142	| `test_unpaired_write_caught` | a raw `+=` to a reservoir bypassing `transfer_quanta`/`commit_flux` (AST audit) | CI fails |
   143	
   144	**Design contradictions retired:** the design's `τ_mass ~ 1e-9` (§6.6) is now provably beaten — mass closes *exactly*; the `1e-9`/`1e-12` tolerances Rev-2 derived apply only to the *derived-energy physics arm*, not to mass. The whole "reservoir precision" question collapses: mass is exact.

   148	## 2. One canonical segment layout — slot-0 identity sentinel (reconciles Rev-2 §4.2 vs §9)
   149	
   150	**Closes:** Codex **#4** (Rev-2 §4.2 defined `S_max` real segments with root *self-parent*; #9 defined a slot-0 sentinel with reals in `[1,S_max)` — two incompatible layouts, two parent conventions, capacity 16 vs 15), Codex **#9** indexing half, blocker **B2** (publish ONE layout + one root-parent convention), the gather half of blocker **B8**, and new-problem **NP-1** (lifecycle eager-gather at `free_rank=-1`). Strengthens Law 4 (single canonical representation).
   151	
   152	### 2.1 The one layout (replaces both Rev-2 §4.2 and the §9 sentinel scheme)
   153	
   154	- **Segment-axis extent `S_slot = S_max + 1 = 17`**, with `S_max = 16` (donor `MaxParts`, `SwimEval.cs:72`). Slot **0 is a frozen identity sentinel**; **real segments occupy slots `[1..S_max]` — full `S_max=16` real capacity** (no capacity loss; Codex's "capacity 15" objection is void because the sentinel is an *extra* slot, not a stolen one).
   155	- Segment tensors are `[W, N_cap, S_slot, …]`, viewed `[B, S_slot, …]` (`B=W·N_cap`) for the kernel. `SEG_AXIS = 2`.
   156	- **Sentinel content (slot 0, immutable):** `pos=(0,0,0)`, `rot=(0,0,0,1)` (bit-exact identity), `mass=0`, `ma=0`, `areaZ=0`, `depth=-1`, `seg_mask=False`. It is never a real segment and never written by development or the depth-scan.
   157	- **Parent convention (single):** `seg_parent[b,k] ∈ [0, S_slot)` is a **creature-local** slot index. A **root's parent = slot 0** (the sentinel), **never −1, never self.** Self-parent (Rev-2 §4.2) is deleted; global `body_base+local` is deleted; `−1` (Rev-2 §9 root sentinel value) is deleted. Every parent gather `pos[b, seg_parent[b,k]]` is in-bounds and, for a root, returns the identity sentinel → `pPos=0, pRot=identity`, bit-matching `SwimEval.cs:669` (`parentIndex<0 → pPos=0, pRot=identity`).
   158	- **Empty body:** all real slots `seg_mask=False`; `tail_slot = 0` (sentinel). No real segment ⇒ every gather targets a valid slot; the tail gather returns the sentinel.
   159	
   160	`root self-parent + hasJoint=False` (Rev-2 §4.2) and `parent=-1` (Rev-2 §9) are **both replaced** by `parent=0`. `seg_hasJoint` still encodes "carries an actuator" (`False` for roots, matching `SwimEval.cs:210` `parentIndex>=0`), but it is no longer load-bearing for the gather — the sentinel makes the gather safe unconditionally.
   161	
   162	### 2.2 Depth-scan and reductions on the one layout
   163	
   164	- Depth-scan: `L=6` passes over `depth ∈ {0..5}` (`MaxDepth=5`, `SwimEval.cs:73`), writing slots where `seg_depth==d & seg_mask`. The sentinel (`depth=-1, mask=False`) is never selected and stays identity; every real parent (strictly lower depth) is resolved in an earlier pass. Static 6 passes, single-valued.
   165	- Reductions: `masked_segment_sum(vals, seg_mask)` = `(vals*seg_mask).sum(SEG_AXIS)` (Rev-2 §3, unchanged). Sentinel contributes exactly 0 (mask False + neutral content). **Finite-padding invariant** (Rev-2 §3) still required: padded/sentinel lanes carry finite neutral values so `0*NaN` cannot leak.
   166	
   167	### 2.3 Lifecycle free-slot gather — no index is ever −1 (closes NP-1)
   168	
   169	Rev-2 §4.4 computed `free_rank = cumsum(free)-1`, which is `−1` for the first slot when it is occupied, and then gathered `payload[free_rank]` *before* `where(claim,…)` masked it — a `-1` gather returns the last row (the eager-gather bug). Fix: the gather index is guaranteed valid by construction.
   170	
   171	```python
   172	free       = ~alive                                   # [W,N_cap]
   173	free_rank  = torch.cumsum(free.to(torch.int64), 1) - 1
   174	n_birth_w  = torch.minimum(request_w, free.sum(1))    # overflow dropped deterministically, logged
   175	claim      = free & (free_rank < n_birth_w[:,None])
   176	gather_idx = free_rank.clamp_min(0)                   # ALWAYS in [0,N_cap): occupied slots (free_rank=-1) map to 0,
   177	                                                      #   their payload is discarded by `claim` below
   178	new_field  = torch.gather(payload_field, 1, gather_idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand_as(field))
   179	field      = torch.where(claim.unsqueeze(-1), new_field, field)   # only genuine births write
   180	```
   350	
   351	**Gate `test_single_source_of_truth`:** AST/audit — `energy`/`struct_N` appear as a stored field **only** in `ColonyState`; no `reservoirs['energy']`/`['struct_N']` key exists; `E_chem` is only ever computed as `e_N * struct_N` (never assigned to a stored pool). Plus `test_snapshot_roundtrip_bit_identical` (Rev-2 §14.3) on the deduplicated schema.
   352	
   353	---
   354	
   355	## 6. One energy equation (reconciles Rev-2 #1 vs #15; `U_cl` consistent; deletes the naive gate)
   356	
   357	**Closes:** Codex **#1** (the combined identity reverted to signed `tFin·U`; the S3 gate re-asserted the invalid `ΔKE=(p_in−wake−drag)dt` that omits `½vᵀΔMv`), Codex **#15 STILL-BROKEN** (structural chemical energy absent from `Σ_stored` ⇒ mint on death; `S3-ENERGY-3` reinstated the very whole-body equation #1 rejected), blocker **B5** (reconcile `U` vs `U_cl`; delete the contradictory S3 equation), and new-problems **NP-7** (energy graph omits structural-body chemical energy — a mint) and **NP-8** (mechanics-vs-ecology energy definitions contradict). Strengthens Law 1 (fidelity), Law 2 (books close), Law 3 (form is function).
   358	
   359	### 6.1 The authoritative locomotion power input `p_in` — `U_cl` consistent (fixes the sign contradiction)
   360	
   361	Ground truth: the donor's two closure identities are **`InputPower = m_t·U·V_t·W_t ≡ tReact·U + pWake`** (reactive, *signed* `U`, `SwimEval.cs:336,358`) and **`CirculatoryInputPower = F_n·V_t ≡ tFin·U_cl + pFin`** (fin, *clamped* `U_cl = max(0,U)`, `SwimEval.cs:395,426`). The fin channel *cannot* close with signed `U` — the clamp `max(0,U)` is the modeling choice "no lift/wake swimming backward" (`SwimEval.cs:395,756,764`). Therefore the **one authoritative input power** is:
   362	
   363	```
   364	U_cl = max(0, U)
   365	p_in = ( tReact·U + pWake )  +  ( tFin·U_cl + pFin )        # reactive uses signed U; fin uses U_cl
   366	```
   367	
   368	**`tFin·U_cl` (not `tFin·U`) everywhere.** This replaces Rev-2 line 65 (the "documentation-only" combined identity that wrote `+ tFin·U`) **and** Rev-2 §15.3 line 859 / `F-K` / `F-M` / `S3-ENERGY-3` (all of which wrote `tFin·U`). The reactive `(a)`-test still uses signed `U`; the fin `(a)`-test still uses `U_cl` (Rev-2 §1(a) was already correct — only the *combined* statements reverted, and those are now fixed). One symbol, one clamp policy, used identically in the S0 gate, the ecology ledger, and the diagnostic.
   369	
   370	### 6.2 The discrete energy-balance gate — the ONLY KE equation (deletes the naive form)
   371	
   372	The naive `ΔKE = (p_in − wake − drag)·dt` is **deleted everywhere it appears** (Rev-2 §1's demoted diagnostic already flagged it; Rev-2 `S3-ENERGY-3` line 921 re-introduced it — that line is removed). The **single** authoritative KE balance is the exact discrete identity for the actual semi-implicit integrator with pose-varying `M_eff` (Rev-2 §1(b)), now written with the constraint impulse `J_c` **and** the regularization impulse `J_reg` (resolution 7):
   373	
   374	```
   375	constrained update:  M_{n+1}(v_{n+1} - v_n) = P + J_c + J_reg,   P = F_stream·Dt,  v_y ≡ 0
   376	ΔKE ≡ ½v_{n+1}ᵀM_{n+1}v_{n+1} − ½v_nᵀM_n v_n
   377	    = v_mid·(P + J_c + J_reg) + ½ v_nᵀ(M_{n+1}−M_n) v_n
   378	    = v_mid·(F_stream·Dt + J_reg) + ½ v_nᵀ ΔM v_n           # J_c·v_mid = 0 (workless: v_y≡0)
   379	R_step = ΔKE − v_mid·(F_stream·Dt + J_reg) − ½ v_nᵀ ΔM v_n   # ≈ 0 by construction
   380	```
   381	
   382	`F_stream = (tReact+tFin)·f̂ + fDrag` (`SwimEval.cs:804`). The `½vᵀΔMv` added-mass term is **mandatory** (its omission was Codex #1's and #15's shared error). `J_c` (vertical plane reaction, `SwimEval.cs:807`) is workless. `v_mid·J_reg` (regularization work) is booked into the numerical-residual energy ledger (resolution 7) so the books close even when regularization fires; on the normal path `J_reg=0` and `R_step≈0`. **This single `R_step` gate is used by S0 (`discrete_balance_1e5`) and inherited unchanged by S3 (`S3-ENERGY-3` now *references* it rather than restating a naive form).** Threshold: `|R_step|/max(KE_n,ε) < 1e-6` (f64 arm) / `< 1e-3` (f32 hot), gated on the drift curve being bounded-oscillating (Rev-2 §1(b)).
   383	
   384	### 6.3 The one reserve→outflow chain (mechanics = ecology; closes NP-7, NP-8, #15)
   385	
   386	There is **one** energy accounting, consumed identically by the kernel (S0/S2) and the economy (S3). Per creature per step:
   387	
   388	```
   389	reserve draw (F-M):   ΔE_reserve = −(P_basal + p_in/η)·dt          P_basal = B0·M^α (Kleiber, α≈0.79)
   390	of which p_in/η:      p_in·dt          -> mechanical work delivered to the body–fluid system
   391	                      (1/η − 1)·p_in·dt -> muscle-inefficiency heat  -> E_heat
   392	                      P_basal·dt        -> basal heat                -> E_heat
   393	mechanical p_in·dt then partitions (the §6.2 physics balance):
   394	                      ΔE_KE            = v_mid·F_stream·Dt + ½vᵀΔMv  (the R_step≈0 identity)
   395	                      (pWake+pFin+wDrag)·dt -> shed wake + fin wake + axial drag -> E_heat
   396	```
   397	
   398	`p_in`, `pWake`, `pFin`, `wDrag`, `ΔE_KE`, `½vᵀΔMv`, `v_mid·J_reg` are the **StepLedger fluxes** the S0 kernel emits (`SwimEval.cs:814` accumulates `(pWake+pFin+wDrag)·dt` as MechWork; S0 additionally emits `ΔE_KE` and the added-mass term). S3 consumes these *authoritative* values — it never redefines the KE relation. This is the single equation NP-8 demanded: `p_in` (muscle→fluid) ≠ COM thrust work `f̂·v_mid·(tReact+tFin)` — they differ by exactly the wake/entrainment energy `(pWake+pFin)` — and the ledger accounts for the difference explicitly, so mechanics (#1) and ecology (#15) cannot disagree.
   399	
   400	### 6.4 Structural chemical energy — counted once, no mint (closes #15, NP-7)
   401	
   402	Codex #15/NP-7: grazing credited `struct_N` and reserve, then death converted *unaccounted* `struct_N` into energetic `Bd`, minting `e_N·struct_N`. Fixed by resolutions 5 + 1 together:
   403	
   404	- **`struct_N` energy is DERIVED, never independently stored:** `E_chem(struct) = e_N · struct_N` (a readout, resolution 5). It **IS part of `Σ_stored`**: `Σ_stored = E_chem(Bp+Bd+Bm derived) + E_chem(struct_N derived) + E_reserve + E_KE`.
   405	- **Assimilation (grazing, F-G):** mass `field −= I_bio; struct_N += AE·I_bio; Bd += (1−AE)·I_bio` — all exact int64 mass moves (resolution 1). The associated energy is *automatically* moved because `E_chem` is `e_N ×` those exact masses. No independent energy write ⇒ no mint.
   406	- **Death:** `struct_N → Bd` is one **exact int64 mass transfer** (resolution 1). Because `E_chem` is derived from mass, the structural energy transfers to `Bd` **exactly and once** — the mint is impossible by construction (there is no second stored copy to double-count).
   407	
   408	**Master balance (open system; replaces Rev-2 §15.4 with the exact-integer bookkeeping arm):**
   409	```
   410	INV-ENERGY (bookkeeping, EXACT int64):
   411	  Σ_stored_q(t) == Σ_stored_q(0) + E_sun_cum_q − E_heat_cum_q − E_export_cum_q
   412	  where the stored/meter energy quanta close by resolution 1's exact integer equality
   413	INV-ENERGY (physics consistency, f32 tolerance):
   414	  the E_KE term (f32 kernel) tracks the §6.2 R_step balance to τ_E_phys=1e-6/step, 1e-4 drift, bounded-oscillating
   415	```

     1	# Codex — Round 3 Review of Rev-3 (reconciled + determinism-relaxed)
     2	
     3	**Date:** 2026-07-12 · Codex `gpt-5.6-sol`, high reasoning, read-only. (Full trace: `codex-rev3-raw.txt`.)
     4	**Overall verdict: NOT APPROVABLE to begin S0** — but the reconciliation and the determinism relaxation both succeeded; the remaining failures are concentrated in the conservation/energy **math**.
     5	
     6	## What round 3 confirms worked
     7	
     8	- **The reconciliation succeeded.** The cross-fix-set contradictions from round 2 are gone: **#4 layout RESOLVED, #9 empty-indexing RESOLVED, #18 firewall RESOLVED, #19 benchmark RESOLVED, #20 risk register RESOLVED**; new-problems **NP-1, NP-2, NP-4, NP-5, NP-9, NP-10, NP-11 RESOLVED**; blockers **B2, B3, B6, B8 RESOLVED.**
     9	- **The determinism relaxation is accepted.** Codex, verbatim: *"The relaxation itself does not break either retained gate… atomic or reassociated float physics cannot alter an already paired int64 debit/credit… Philox's integer core… remains independent of float-kernel scheduling."* One caveat: Tier-2 must be stated as *reproducible raw draws / discrete decisions* — the float Box–Muller transform (`log/sqrt/sin/cos`) is not itself bit-exact under relaxed float.
    10	
    11	Round-2-residual score: **5 RESOLVED · 6 PARTIAL · 4 STILL-BROKEN.**
    12	
    13	## What's still broken — all in the two retained hard gates (conservation + mechanics)
    14	
    15	1. **Regularization sign error (STILL-BROKEN; #2, #12, NP-3, B4).** From `(M+rI)Δv=P` ⇒ `MΔv=P−r·Δv`, so the booked impulse must be `J_reg = −r·Δv`; Rev-3 wrote `+r·Δv` and built both the momentum and the `R_step` energy gate on it. Elementary, but it makes both gates false whenever regularization fires.
    16	2. **Energy quanta are non-commensurate (STILL-BROKEN; #5, #15, B1).** With mass quantum `1e-9` and energy quantum `1e-3` and energy density `e_N`, one mass quantum ≈ **3.581** energy quanta — not an integer — so structural chemical energy cannot enter an *exact* int64 energy equality. Exact-integer conservation works cleanly for a single currency but breaks at the mass↔energy conversion.
    17	3. **Reserve economy has no inflow (STILL-BROKEN; #15).** Rev-3 removed the grazing reserve-credit to stop the double-mint but supplied no replacement partition, so reserve only decreases — no valid feeding path.
    18	4. **Conservation-mechanism gaps (B1, new problems).** `transfer_quanta` permits `n > src` → negative reservoirs while "conserving" the total (needs an availability cap); no int64 overflow bound; death-order hazard (if `alive` is cleared before `struct_N→Bd`, mass vanishes from `close_books` — transaction ordering unspecified).
    19	5. **RNG detail bugs (PARTIAL; #13, NP-6).** The rejection-continuation has no free counter bits for the `attempt` index; the Box–Muller endpoint test uses `2^24−1` where the upper endpoint needs `2^32−1`.
    20	6. **Two untouched round-2 items (#17):** mutation rates still unnamed; the "genotype mutation preserves `S_max`" wording (should be `N_max/E_max`) unfixed.
    21	7. **gain1 fixtures (PARTIAL; #8, B7):** analytic method given, but no frozen input vectors / expected values / specified quadrature.
    22	
    23	## Internal-consistency audit: FAIL (4 real contradictions)
    24	
    25	- **§7.1 vs §7.3:** §7.1 says production uses the exact *unregularized* solve for every conservation gate; §7.3 says production regularizes. Both can't govern the same step.
    26	- **§1.3 vs §§6–7:** §1.3 deletes `R_num` and its residual mechanism; §§6–7 then book regularization work into a "numerical-residual energy ledger" that no longer exists in the table/schema.
    27	- **§0 vs §5:** §0 disclaims long-run float replay; §5 still says forcing-completeness makes resumed execution *bit-identical*. (A leftover from before the §0 relaxation — needs "serialization is bit-preserving; execution replay is not.")
    28	- **Pre-flight checklist is circular:** it says implementation tests must be green "before S0 code begins," but those tests require S0 code. They are S0 *acceptance* gates, not pre-code gates.
    29	
    30	## Codex's required changes before approval
    31	
    32	1. `J_reg = −r·Δv`; repair `R_step`; publish one solver branch selecting exact / regularized / invalid-zero.
    33	2. Redesign exact energy representation: commensurate quanta or an integer conversion scheme; explicit `E_KE` + regularization-work treatment; checked overflow; source caps; death-transaction ordering.
    34	3. Specify a non-minting feeding partition that replenishes reserve while counting structural energy exactly once.
    35	4. Real rejection-continuation address; correct its test/probability claim.
    36	5. Freeze concrete gain1 fixture inputs/expected/quadrature/tolerances.
    37	6. Finish the two untouched #17 defects.
    38	7. Replace the vague feeding stub with a fixed op/tensor/byte spec; require a non-OOM authorization measurement.
    39	
    40	> "Until those are fixed, the plan's top two claimed hard gates — exact conservation and correct mechanics — are not real."

It plans **repo scaffolding → the S0/SpikeSwim go/no-go spike → the S1/S2 keystone slices → the S3–S9 milestone contracts → the verification/salvage/language protocol → sequencing, risks, and definition-of-done.** It does not re-open settled design decisions (§2.7–2.9, §4.8, §6, §7); where a "why" is needed it cross-references the design doc rather than restating it.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-implementation-plan.md:9:The build is **depth-first**: nothing in a later slice begins until the prior slice's gate is green **on a telemetry artifact** (P7, §7.1.4 — no claim from code inspection). Three hard phase gates structure it: **(A) Prove the substrate** (scaffold + S0 SpikeSwim clears four gates at H1/H2), **(B) Close the books** (S1→S2→S3→S4, energy and mass ledgers close end-to-end), **(C) Emergence & embodiment** (S5→S9, each with a falsifiable acceptance criterion). "Done" is judged the same way everywhere: conservation ledger green, reproducible-within-seed on a fixed device, no capability read from a stat vector, no green-keeping `gain=0` dial, and the deciding number recorded in a telemetry artifact — never asserted from source.

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-implementation-plan.md:570:**Components:** `graze(pos,C_form,field,cfg,dt)→(I_assim,egesta,field_debit)` Holling-II, `I_bio←min(I_bio,D_local)`, `C_form` from morphology (§6.4); `metabolism(mass,temp,cfg)→P_basal` Kleiber α≈0.79, `P_active=P_basal+P_loco` (SwimEval muscle power); `reproduce(...)` mass-scaled eligibility, paired debit==credit `(E,struct_N)` parent→child, juvenile body from genome (real `M_off`, never a flat tank, §6.5.2); egesta `(1−AE)·I_bio` and death-routed `struct_N`→`Bd`; birth/death into `(W,N_cap)` via free-slot claim / alive-mask clear (deterministic); telemetry: per-cause mortality, lifespan, reserve histograms, `[CONS]` energy line.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-implementation-plan.md:572:**Acceptance (falsifiable):** in one deterministic run a cohort **feeds→grows→reproduces→dies with energy books closed end-to-end** (`INV-ENERGY < τ_energy`/close, drift bounded-oscillating over ≥1e6 steps); every transfer passes `INV-TRANSFER<1e-6`; population **sustains (plateaus at production÷real-per-capita-intake) without any `carryingCap`/`energyCap` knob** (§6.8–6.9). **Top risk RK-13:** loop oscillates/tuning-fragile → single-dial activation with ledger watched; a collapse is a diagnostic that fidelity was dropped upstream, never fixed by retuning the Joule/SMR anchor.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-implementation-plan.md:578:**Components:** `find` two-sided multi-modal detection `Detect(modality)` vs `Signature` across {vision↔light, smell↔chemical, lateral-line↔flow, electro↔electric}, no dominant modality; `close` pursuit from real drag/yaw-torque + burst gear, not a speed stat; `seize` two-sided `GripRate` vs `Evade`, overpower = mass ratio, `F_crack` from jaw geometry; `consume` gated `CanCapture∧CanDigest`, one paired transaction prey `(E,struct_N)`→predator(AE)+detritus(1−AE); spatial-hash neighbor reuse (no cell-bucketed prey mass); telemetry: trophic occupancy, kill/attempt, per-modality stats, mass-flow ledger.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-implementation-plan.md:604:**Acceptance (falsifiable):** **render never feeds fitness** — a deterministic run produces byte-identical (same-device) sim numbers with viewer attached and detached, and the viewer reproduces a checkpointed run from `ColonyState` alone. Any headless-vs-viewer divergence fails the slice. **Top risk:** accidental coupling → the one-way `observe` import boundary + render-off equivalence gate make any leak a CI failure.

Every per-segment computation writes a neutral value into padded lanes: padded `seg_mass=0`, `seg_ma=0`, and any lane produced by a division/`quat` op is guarded so padded rows carry finite defaults (this dovetails with the #9 sentinel-row fix for root/empty gathers). docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:700:**What Codex is right about.** `transfer(src,dst,a)` written as `src -= a; dst += a` in **f32** does *not* preserve the total: subtracting `0.5` from `1.0` is exact, but adding `0.5` to `1e8` rounds away (f32 ULP at 1e8 is ~8). The debit is realized, the credit is lost → a structural mint/leak. No compensated f64 sum *afterward* recovers information already destroyed in the f32 write. So plan:229, plan:414–425, plan:459–471 ("`residual==0`", "`<1e-9`", "million-step `<1e-6`") are unsupported **for f32 reservoir state**.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:706:Every *registered reservoir* — the quantity `close_books()` sums — is stored **float64**:
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:711:| `struct_N`, `energy` (reserve `E`), `mass` | `[W,N_cap]` | f32 → **f64** |
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:715:The **hot physics stays f32** (swim kernel `pos/rot/vcom/M_eff/dv` per plan:174–178, §4.8). The seam: the f32 kernel *emits fluxes* (a locomotion-power draw, a drag-dissipation credit); those f32 flux amounts are cast up to f64 (an **exact** widening — f32 ⊂ f64, zero rounding) before touching any reservoir. Rationale for f64 *state* is **dynamic range, not conservation** (§5.2 already gives exact conservation): f64 keeps the residual reservoir at true machine-noise level (a small flux onto a large pool lands in the pool, not in the residual). At f64, `1e8 + 0.5` is exact (ULP ≈ 1.5e-8), so a physically-realizable flux never vanishes below the reservoir ULP unless the magnitude ratio exceeds ~1e15 — which no physical flux does.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:736:After the write, `src_exact + dst_exact + R_num` equals the pre-write `src + dst + R_num` **to the precision at which `R_num` itself accumulates** (O(eps²)). `R_num` is registered as a real reservoir and is included in `close_books()`. This is not a "sanctioned wrapper hiding a nondeterministic op": TwoSum is pure elementwise f64 arithmetic; the debit/credit still use the masked-axis-sum layout (canonical `[B,S_max]`+mask / grid stencils), never `scatter_add`.

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:75:**Closes:** Codex **#5 STILL-BROKEN** (the f64 TwoSum residual had the wrong sign, `R_num -= (e_s+e_d)` where closure needs `+=`), blocker **B1** (fix the residual sign + specify a deterministic reduction of the error terms), and new-problem **NP-9** (f64-reservoir vs "O(W) f64 islands" inconsistency). Strengthens Law 2 (the books close) and Law 4 (single canonical representation).
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:77:**Root decision.** The wrong-sign TwoSum is not patched — it is **deleted**. Conserved *reservoir state* is stored as **int64 fixed-point quanta**, not f64. Integer transfers are exact by construction: there is no rounding, therefore no residual, therefore no sign to get wrong. Physics and rates stay f32 (hot loop) / f64 (Lamb quadrature, oracle arm) exactly as #11 specifies; **only the conserved reservoir totals are int64.** This makes `close_books()` an *exact integer equality* rather than a tolerance, and makes the reduction of totals order-independent (integer addition is associative and exact — deterministic on any device regardless of reduction tree), which is what B1's "deterministic reduction of the error terms" asks for: there are no error terms.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:86:Quanta are declared once in `core/config.py` (`QUANTUM = {'mass': 1e-9, 'energy': 1e-3}`) and are part of `config_hash` (determinism depends on them). A reservoir tensor's dtype is **int64**; its physical value is `quanta * q`.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md:96:**Biomass mass/energy reservoir totals (`Bp`, `E_chem`, and the biomass part of any mass sum) are NOT stored here** — they are *derived masked sums* over ColonyState per resolution 5. `energy` (reserve) and `struct_N` live in ColonyState as int64 (resolution 5), and are summed into the ledger as derived totals. docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:698:### FINDING #5 — The f64 reservoir ledger + explicit numerical-residual reservoir + scale-dependent tolerances
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:700:**What Codex is right about.** `transfer(src,dst,a)` written as `src -= a; dst += a` in **f32** does *not* preserve the total: subtracting `0.5` from `1.0` is exact, but adding `0.5` to `1e8` rounds away (f32 ULP at 1e8 is ~8). The debit is realized, the credit is lost → a structural mint/leak. No compensated f64 sum *afterward* recovers information already destroyed in the f32 write. So plan:229, plan:414–425, plan:459–471 ("`residual==0`", "`<1e-9`", "million-step `<1e-6`") are unsupported **for f32 reservoir state**.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:706:Every *registered reservoir* — the quantity `close_books()` sums — is stored **float64**:
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:713:| `R_num` (numerical-residual reservoir, §5.3) | scalar per `(W, currency)` | **f64** |
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:715:The **hot physics stays f32** (swim kernel `pos/rot/vcom/M_eff/dv` per plan:174–178, §4.8). The seam: the f32 kernel *emits fluxes* (a locomotion-power draw, a drag-dissipation credit); those f32 flux amounts are cast up to f64 (an **exact** widening — f32 ⊂ f64, zero rounding) before touching any reservoir. Rationale for f64 *state* is **dynamic range, not conservation** (§5.2 already gives exact conservation): f64 keeps the residual reservoir at true machine-noise level (a small flux onto a large pool lands in the pool, not in the residual). At f64, `1e8 + 0.5` is exact (ULP ≈ 1.5e-8), so a physically-realizable flux never vanishes below the reservoir ULP unless the magnitude ratio exceeds ~1e15 — which no physical flux does.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:736:After the write, `src_exact + dst_exact + R_num` equals the pre-write `src + dst + R_num` **to the precision at which `R_num` itself accumulates** (O(eps²)). `R_num` is registered as a real reservoir and is included in `close_books()`. This is not a "sanctioned wrapper hiding a nondeterministic op": TwoSum is pure elementwise f64 arithmetic; the debit/credit still use the masked-axis-sum layout (canonical `[B,S_max]`+mask / grid stencils), never `scatter_add`.
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:745:    total   = pairwise_sum_f64(registered_reservoirs[currency]) + R_num[currency]   # compensated
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:754:**Tolerance derivation (scale-dependent, defensible — this is the derivation Codex demanded).** The reservoirs are **all-positive** (mass and stored energy ≥ 0), so the summation condition number `κ = Σ|R_i|/|ΣR_i| = 1` — no catastrophic cancellation. A pairwise-compensated f64 reduction over `N_elem` cells then has relative error bounded by:
docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md:775:test_transfer_exact_twosum   :  after N random transfers, Σreservoirs + R_num == I0 to 1e-14 rel   [f64]
