   313	## 5. Single source of truth for `energy` / `struct_N` (closes Codex #14 duplication)
   314	
   315	**Closes:** Codex **#14** (`SimulationSnapshot` duplicated `energy`/`struct_N` in both `colony` and `reservoirs`; omitted external-forcing state for snapshot-alone replay; required `safetensors` without pinning) and new-problem **NP-10** (duplicate authoritative state). Strengthens Law 4 — *"if you are writing code to keep two copies of one thing in sync, you have already lost."*
   316	
   317	**Decision.** Per-creature `energy` (reserve, J) and `struct_N` (structural nutrient, mol N) are **canonical in ColonyState and stored nowhere else** (int64 quanta per resolution 1). The biomass mass/energy reservoir *totals* are **derived masked sums** over creatures, computed on demand for `close_books` and never stored:
   318	
   319	```python
   320	# derived, never stored — resolution 1 provides exact int64 sums
   321	biomass_struct_total_q = int64_masked_sum(colony.struct_N_q * alive_mask)   # mass currency
   322	biomass_reserve_total_q = int64_masked_sum(colony.energy_q  * alive_mask)   # energy currency
   323	E_chem_creature = e_N * (colony.struct_N_q * q_mass)          # DERIVED readout [J], not stored
   324	```
   325	
   326	The **abiotic** reservoirs (`Nd` dissolved nutrient, `Bd` detritus, `Sed` sediment) are field-level `[W,G,G,B]`/`[W,G,G]` int64 and remain in `reservoirs` — they are genuinely distinct quantities, not duplicates. `energy`/`struct_N` are **removed from `reservoirs`** entirely; `close_books` reads the derived creature totals plus the abiotic reservoirs.
   327	
   328	**Canonical `SimulationSnapshot` (deduplicated; replaces Rev-2 §14.1):**
   329	```
   330	SimulationSnapshot = {
   331	  header,                 # schema_version, git_sha, torch/cuda, device, dtypes, config_hash, quanta
   332	  config,                 # full Config + WorldConfig (+ QUANTUM table)
   333	  clock,                  # {now, step, dt, scale, forcing_phase}
   334	  colony,                 # ColonyState: alive, stable_id, parent_id, generation, pos/heading/vel,
   335	                          #   energy_q (int64 J-quanta), struct_N_q (int64 mol-quanta),   <-- SOLE COPY
   336	                          #   genome_ptr, age, species_tag, developed-body segs [·,S_slot,·]+seg_mask
   337	  genotype,               # [P,N_max/E_max] pool
   338	  reservoirs,             # abiotic int64 ONLY: Nd,Bd,Sed; energy meters E_sun/E_heat/E_export;
   339	                          #   flux carries (f64, §1.4).  NO energy/struct_N here (single source of truth)
   340	  allocator,              # {next_eid[W] int64}   (resolution 4; no host scalar)
   341	  innovation,             # {next_iid[W or global], structural_key_cache}
   342	  forcing,                # stateful external forcing (S6 advected buffers; empty pre-S6) -- REPLAY-COMPLETE
   343	  rng                     # {master_seed}   (counter-based -> no PRNG buffer)
   344	}
   345	```
   346	
   347	**External-forcing completeness (closes the #14 "snapshot-alone omits external actions" gap):** `forcing` carries every stateful external driver (S6 current/advection buffers; any scripted forcing schedule) so a run resumed from the snapshot alone is bit-identical — the negative gate `test_colonystate_alone_is_insufficient` (Rev-2 §14.3) is retained and now also fails if `forcing` is stripped.
   348	
   349	**Serializer pinned (closes the #14 "requires safetensors without pinning"):** add `safetensors >= 0.4.3` to the pinned dependency table (the one artifact-level table of Codex #16). Tensors → `tensors.safetensors` (int64 reservoirs/quanta serialize losslessly and deterministically — no float ordering hazard); scalars/registries/config → `meta.json`. `torch.save`/pickle forbidden.
   350	
   351	**Gate `test_single_source_of_truth`:** AST/audit — `energy`/`struct_N` appear as a stored field **only** in `ColonyState`; no `reservoirs['energy']`/`['struct_N']` key exists; `E_chem` is only ever computed as `e_N * struct_N` (never assigned to a stored pool). Plus `test_snapshot_roundtrip_bit_identical` (Rev-2 §14.3) on the deduplicated schema.

   355	## 6. One energy equation (reconciles Rev-2 #1 vs #15; `U_cl` consistent; deletes the naive gate)
   497	## 9. Throughput / benchmark reconciliation (Codex #6, #19, #20)
   498	
   499	**Closes:** Codex **#6** (G-E2E ordering: "post-S2" yet needs S3 feeding; S0 headroom double-counts population growth), Codex **#19** (`vram_cap` unresolved; Stage-4 required a *flattened prototype* despite that representation being deferred; compile-warmup-failure unspecified), Codex **#20** (marked churn/static-capture + conservation "RETIRED" while the birth gather was unsafe, layouts conflicted, and the residual had the wrong sign), and the hardware half of blocker **B8**. Strengthens Law "measure, don't assert."
   500	
   501	### 9.1 G-E2E ordering resolved — stubbed-feeding whole tick (fixes the #6 contradiction)
   502	
   503	Rev-2 §6.5 placed G-E2E "post-S2" but listed an S3-feeding dependency — contradictory. **Decision: G-E2E runs at the S2→S3 boundary on a *whole tick with a feeding STUB*** (a deterministic fixed-cost placeholder with representative memory-traffic, not real assimilation), then is **re-confirmed post-S3 with real feeding.** This removes the dependency contradiction: G-E2E needs S2 (StepLive + development + ≥1 field + spatial hash) plus a feeding stub — all available at the S2→S3 boundary. The floor `F_sci=2.31e7` c-steps/s and the emitted measured `φ_loco` are unchanged; the stub run is labeled provisional, the post-S3 run authoritative.
   504	
   505	### 9.2 S0 headroom double-count corrected (fixes #6)
   506	
   507	`F_sci = N_pop·T_run/W_budget = 1000·1e9/43200 = 2.31e7` c-steps/s uses `N_pop = N_cap = 1000` (**worst case, already at capacity**). Rev-2 §6.3 then multiplied by a `1.5×` "pop growth toward N_cap + world densification" factor — which **double-counts**, because `F_sci` is *already* evaluated at `N_cap`. That factor is removed:
   508	
   509	```
   510	F_loco_bare = F_sci / φ_loco = 2.31e7 / 0.5 = 4.63e7 c-steps/s
   511	headroom    = 1.5 (StepLive over frozen Step: yaw + P-controller + torque)
   512	            × 1.3 (bench↔production variance, thermal throttle, measurement)
   513	            ≈ 1.95×                         # was 2.9× (the 1.5 pop-growth factor removed)
   514	F_loco_S0   = 4.63e7 × 1.95 ≈ 9.0e7 creature-steps/s     <-- corrected S0 gate (d) floor
   515	```
   516	At near-term batch `B = W·N_cap ≈ 1024`, `9.0e7` c-steps/s ⇒ **≤ 11.4 µs/full-articulated-step** — a looser, honest target than the double-counted `1.4e8` (7.3 µs). The `256-world×1000×120 = 3.07e7` figure remains the S8-era many-worlds realtime target only. Gate-(d) text (Rev-2 §6.4) updated: floor = `F_loco_S0 = 9.0e7`.
   517	
   518	### 9.3 No flattened prototype required (fixes #19)
   519	
   520	Rev-2 §19.2 Stage 4 required a *flattened prototype* to measure masking tax — but the flattened/arena layout is a **deferred** optimization (Rev-2 §4.6). **Decision: Stage 4 is replaced by an *analytic + same-layout* masking-tax estimate.** The `S_slot/mean_seg ≈ 17/6 ≈ 2.8×` waste is reported analytically, and cross-checked empirically by timing the canonical `[B,S_slot]` kernel at the H1 mean-segment count vs at full `S_slot` occupancy (both on the *same* padded layout — no separate flattened implementation built). This keeps the deferred representation deferred (Law 7, grow-on-demand) while still quantifying the tax. Stage 4 cell count: 2 (same-layout occupancy sweep), 0 prototype builds.
   521	
   522	### 9.4 Hardware pins parameterized (fixes #19; hardware half of B8)
   523	
   524	The following are explicit owner-supplied parameters, **not** resolved here:
   525	```
   526	vram_cap  = 11 GiB              # RTX 5070 = 12 GiB GDDR7; ~11 usable; cell over cap -> 'OOM-skip', not fail
   527	gpu_model = "RTX 5070"          # Blackwell; ~30 TFLOP FP32, FP64 ~1:64 -> keep f64 minimal (LambK + validation)
   528	cpu_model = "Ryzen 7 8700F"     # Zen 4, 8 cores / 16 threads; CPU-rung baseline = 16 threads
   529	```
   530	`bench.py`'s `vram_cap` (Rev-2 §19.3 `"<PIN Q#1>"`) reads this pin; a cell exceeding it is recorded `OOM-skip` (logs `max_memory_allocated`), never a gate-(d) fail. **Compile-warmup-failure behavior (Rev-2 gap):** if r1/r2 warmup fails to reach the `<2%` step-time convergence within `max 10` iters, the cell is recorded `warmup-fail` and **falls back to the r0 (eager) number for that (device,B)** with a logged flag — it is not silently dropped and does not fail the gate; the ladder (§5.8) then decides on the r0 number.
   531	
   532	### 9.5 Risks stay ACTIVE until verified in code (fixes #20)
   533	
   534	Codex #20: Rev-2 §20.1 marked "deterministic ragged reduction," "flattened-churn static capture," and "f32 conservation drift" **RETIRED** — but the birth gather was unsafe (NP-1), the layouts conflicted (#4), and the residual had the wrong sign (#5). **Decision: reclassify all three from "RETIRED" to "resolved-in-spec / verify-in-code (ACTIVE)".** They remain in the *active* register with mitigation "Rev-3 §2/§1/§4 fix specified; gate green required before close" until the Rev-3 tests (`test_no_negative_gather_index`, `test_close_books_order_independent`, `test_transfer_exact_int`, `test_layout_capacity`, graph-pointer stability T14) pass in code. No risk is retired on the strength of a spec alone. The register's top ranks (energy-gate correctness, end-to-end throughput, gain1 independence, S1 closure realizability) are unchanged.
   535	
   536	### 9.6 Tests / gates
   537	
   538	`test_headroom_no_double_count` (assert the S0 floor derivation contains no factor evaluated twice at `N_cap`). `test_ge2e_dependencies` (G-E2E's dependency set is exactly {S2, feeding-stub}, provisional; re-confirm gate post-S3). `test_bench_no_flattened_build` (AST: the benchmark builds no flattened/arena layout). `test_risk_register_no_premature_retire` (the three §9.5 risks are `ACTIVE` until their named Rev-3 gates are green).

   400	Two measure-first, reversible decisions flagged inline:

   403	
   404	### S1 — Conserved single-nutrient economy (the keystone)
   405	
   406	**Design inversion from the donor:** the donor grew biomass toward a static Perlin cap (`PlanktonField.cs:114`, the #1 mint bug) and discarded remineralized snow (`MarineSnowField.cs:56–58`). S1 makes both structurally impossible: every gram of N moves between tracked reservoirs, `Σ reservoirs = const` (§6.1, §6.6). The donor's *equations* (`NutrientChem.cs`) and its *conservation-test pattern* port forward; its non-conserving *architecture* does not.
   407	
   408	Closed inventory: `I_N = ΣNd + ΣBp + ΣBd + ΣBm + Σ_alive struct_N + ΣSed`. Box closed at S1 (no geology source, no burial sink) → `I_N(t)==I_N(0)` to tolerance.
   409	
   410	#### S1.A — Reservoir model + ledger (build first)
   411	
   412	| Task | Detail | Effort/Risk |
   413	|---|---|---|
   414	| **S1.1** Reservoir registry | Named f32 tensors: `Nd,Bp,Bd,Bm (W,G,G,B)`, `struct_N (W,N_cap)`, `Sed (W,G,G)` (inert at S1). Each registered with name+shape. | S/Low |
   415	| **S1.2** Paired-transfer primitive | `core/ledger.py::transfer(src,dst,amount)` debits/credits by the **identical tensor** via deterministic masked add — the ONLY sanctioned way to move N. | M/Med |
   416	| **S1.3** `close_books()` | f64 compensated sum over all registered reservoirs every N ticks; returns `closure_residual`, emits `[CONS]` line. | S/Low |
   417	
   418	```python
   419	# core/ledger.py
   420	def register_reservoir(name:str, t:Tensor)->None
   421	def transfer(src:Tensor, dst:Tensor, amount:Tensor)->None   # |Δsrc+Δdst|==0 by construction
   422	def close_books()->LedgerResidual                            # f64; asserts INV-MASS/INV-TRANSFER
   423	```
   424	
   425	**Acceptance S1.A:** random `transfer` sequence leaves `close_books().residual==0` (f64); any direct write to a registered reservoir outside `transfer` is caught by an audit test (INV-TRANSFER `<1e-6` f32/op).

   468	
   469	#### S1 Acceptance Gate
   470	
   785	**What Codex is right about.** As written (plan:443–444), S1 has *only* production (`Nd→Bp` until `Nd` exhausts) and remineralization/sinking of `Bd`. There is **no `Bp→Bd/Nd` producer loss**, so when `Nd` empties, production merely *stops* and `Bp` sits flat forever. "Bloom must … crash" (plan:467, plan:473) and "drifters plateau" cannot occur. `Bp` never declines; there are no consumers to plateau.
   786	
   787	**Resolution — I pick: ADD explicit producer respiration + mortality to S1** (the design already wants an in-S1 crash, §6.8: "drawdown empties `Nd` → production collapses → biomass declines"; and the remin loop that receives `Bd` already exists at S1.8). The **consumer-plateau claim moves to S3** (it genuinely needs grazing). Both moves are specified below.
   788	
   789	#### 7.1 New S1 producer-loss processes (INSERT into §S1.C, after S1.7)
   790	
   791	All units mol N; all transfers go through the §5.2 primitive; energy side per #15.
   792	
   793	```
   794	S1.7b  Producer respiration (maintenance):     Bp -> Nd,  releases heat
   795	   P_resp = m_resp · Bp                         [mol N · s⁻¹],  m_resp ≈ 0.10 /day  (anchor: phyto respiration ≈10–30% of gross production, §6.2 NPP band)
   796	   transfer(Bp, Nd, P_resp·dt, 'mass')
   797	   E_heat += e_N · P_resp·dt   ;  E_chem(Bp) tracks -e_N·P_resp·dt   (Nd carries NO energy — inorganic)
   798	
   799	S1.8b  Producer mortality (senescence + crowding):  Bp -> Bd,  energy carried
   800	   M_p = (d0 + d_dd·Bp) · Bp                     [mol N · s⁻¹],  d0 ≈ 0.05/day, d_dd density-dependent
   801	   transfer(Bp, Bd, M_p·dt, 'mass')
   802	   E_chem moves Bp->Bd with the biomass (no heat)
   803	```
   804	
   805	`d_dd·Bp` (crowding) is what gives the crash its bite: at the bloom peak `Bp` is large, mortality is super-linear, biomass collapses even as production has already stalled from `Nd` exhaustion. `M_p` feeds `Bd` → S1.8 remin returns `(1−BGE)` of it to `Nd` (deep) → S1.10 mixing lifts it → **damped re-bloom → steady standing stock**. This is genuine bloom-and-crash, fully conserved, **no cap knob** — the crash is a real loss flux, not a ceiling. `m_resp, d0, d_dd` are frozen *rate anchors* (§1.2), never retuned to hit a target biomass; a failure to stabilize is a diagnostic, not a tuning target (§6.8, §6.9).
   806	
   807	#### 7.2 S1 acceptance rewrite (REPLACES plan:473 item 3)
   808	
   809	> **3.** Blooms/deserts emerge from the loop. A seeded nutrient pulse must: (i) spike production, (ii) draw `Nd` down toward exhaustion, (iii) **crash — `Bp` rises to a peak then declines** as producer respiration+mortality (S1.7b/S1.8b) debit it faster than the stalled production replaces it, (iv) settle to a **damped oscillation / steady standing stock** as mortality→`Bd`→remin→`Nd`→mixing closes the return. **No hand-coded ceiling.** Zonation falls out of the pump. *(The consumer-plateau claim "drifters plateau at production÷intake" MOVES to S3 — it requires grazers, which S1 does not implement.)*
   810	
   811	#### 7.3 S1 test rewrites (REPLACE plan:467, ADD)
   812	
   813	```
   814	test_bloom_self_terminates (rewrite): seeded pulse → Bp(t) is unimodal-then-declining (argmax not at t_end,
   815	                                       and Bp(t_end) < Bp(peak)·0.7); Nd draws down then partially recovers;
   816	                                       close_books < 1e-12 throughout; no baseCap/carryingCap symbol reachable.
   817	test_producer_loss_closes (new):       respiration Bp->Nd and mortality Bp->Bd each pass INV-TRANSFER (|Δsrc+Δdst|<1e-14 f64);
   818	                                       energy: E_chem drop == E_heat rise (respiration) OR E_chem(Bd) rise (mortality).
   819	test_damped_steady_state (new):        1e6-step soak reaches a bounded standing stock with no cap knob; oscillation amplitude decays (not monotone growth, not zero-flat).
   820	```
   821	
   822	The plan-wide claim (plan:9, plan:473, design §6.8) "drifters plateau at production÷intake" is retagged **[S3]** everywhere it appears in S1 contexts.
   823	
   824	---
   825	
   840	```
   841	
   842	`e_N` is the single conversion that lets **derived** chemical energy of any N-pool be read off its mass: `E_chem(pool) = e_N · N_pool` [J]. This is a *readout*, **not a second stored-and-synced scalar** (design §6.1 — storing it independently is the very sync-glue anti-pattern P1 forbids).
   843	
   844	#### 15.2 Reservoirs (all f64, per #5) — the energy currency graph
   845	
   846	| # | Reservoir | Symbol | Unit | Stored or derived | Kind |
   847	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   848	| E1 | Producer/detritus/microbial chemical energy | `E_chem = e_N·(Bp+Bd+Bm)` | J | **derived** from mass pools | internal |
   849	| E2 | Creature metabolic reserve (lipid/glycogen) | `E_reserve = energy[W,N_cap]` | J | **stored** (§6.1: genuinely distinct) | internal |
   850	| E3 | Creature bulk kinetic energy | `E_KE = ½vᵀM_eff v` | J | derived from kernel state (f32) | internal |
   851	| E4 | Absorbed-light input meter | `E_sun_cum` | J | stored, monotone↑ (Kahan) | **INPUT** |
   852	| E5 | Heat / respiration / dissipation sink meter | `E_heat_cum` | J | stored, monotone↑ (Kahan) | **OUTPUT** |
   853	| E6 | Exported (buried) energy meter | `E_export_cum` | J | stored, monotone↑ (off at S1) | **OUTPUT** |
   854	
   855	This is an **open system**: E4 is the one external input (sun), E5+E6 the outputs. `Nd` (dissolved inorganic nutrient) and `Sed` carry **zero energy density** — re-fixing `Nd` re-injects sun energy; this decoupling is what makes the loop open, not a closed constant.
   856	
   857	#### 15.3 Fluxes (transaction equations + units; each is a §5.2 paired transfer or a metered credit)
   858	
   859	Powers in W = J·s⁻¹. `p_in` (muscle→body input power) uses the **cluster-#1-corrected** identity `p_in = tReact·U + pWake + tFin·U + pFin` (consistent with the S0 fix; the plan:345 form omitting `tFin·U` is superseded there).
   860	
   861	```
   862	F-L  Photosynthesis (INPUT):    ΔE_chem = +e_N·ΔBp ;  E_sun_cum += e_N·ΔBp
   863	        (ΔBp from S1.7 production; light energy = the energy needed to fix ΔBp from Nd. Incident I(x,z),
   864	         §3.9 Beer–Lambert, remains an analytic DRIVER, not a reservoir; only ABSORBED-and-fixed light is metered.)
   865	
   866	F-G  Grazing (chem → reserve + egesta):        [S3]
   867	        E_chem(field) -= e_N·I_bio ; E_reserve += AE·e_N·I_bio ; E_chem(Bd) += (1-AE)·e_N·I_bio
   868	        (mass side: field-=I_bio, struct_N+=AE·I_bio, Bd+=(1-AE)·I_bio ; §6.4. No heat: assimilation loss is egesta, mass-conserved.)
   869	
   870	F-M  Metabolism (reserve → heat + mech):        [S3]
   871	        P_basal = B0·M^α  (Kleiber, α≈0.79, §6.5.1)
   872	        E_reserve -= (P_basal + p_in/η)·dt
   873	        E_heat_cum += (P_basal + (1/η - 1)·p_in)·dt        # basal + muscle-inefficiency heat
   874	        E_mech_in  += p_in·dt                               # mechanical work delivered to body
   875	        (identity check: draw = basal + p_in/η = heat + mech ✓  → S3-ENERGY-2)
   876	
   877	F-K  Locomotion (mech → KE + hydro dissipation): [S0/S2 kernel]
   878	        ΔE_KE = ½vᵀM_{n+1}v_{n+1} − ½vᵀM_n v_n
   879	        E_heat_cum += (pWake + pFin + wDrag)·dt             # shed wake + fin wake + axial drag → heat
   880	        (the exact discrete balance incl. the ½vᵀ(M_{n+1}−M_n)v added-mass term is the S0 energy-gate cluster;
   881	         THIS ledger consumes the S0 StepLedger's {p_in, pWake, pFin, wDrag, ΔE_KE} as authoritative flux values.)
   882	
   883	F-R  Bacterial remineralization (chem → microbial + heat):  [S1]
   884	        R = k_remin(z)·Bd
   885	        E_chem(Bd) -= e_N·R·dt ; E_chem(Bm) += e_N·BGE·R·dt ; E_heat_cum += e_N·(1-BGE)·R·dt
   886	        (mass: BGE→Bm, (1-BGE)→Nd, §6.3. The (1-BGE) N returns to Nd but its ENERGY is respired to heat — the key decoupling.)
   887	
   888	F-P  Producer respiration + mortality:          [S1, new §7.1]
   889	        respiration: E_chem(Bp) -= e_N·P_resp·dt ; E_heat_cum += e_N·P_resp·dt
   890	        mortality:   E_chem(Bp) -= e_N·M_p·dt   ; E_chem(Bd) += e_N·M_p·dt   (no heat, biomass carries energy)
   891	
   892	F-B  Burial (OUTPUT, off at S1, on at S9):
   893	        E_export_cum += e_N·w_bury·Bd·dt          (mass: Bd→Sed; energy leaves the system)
   894	```
   895	
   896	#### 15.4 The master balance — "books close" = every flux accounted (NOT total constant)
   897	
   898	```
   899	Σ_stored(t) := E_chem(t) + E_reserve(t) + E_KE(t)
   900	INV-ENERGY:   | Σ_stored(t) − Σ_stored(0) − (E_sun_cum − E_heat_cum − E_export_cum) |  <  τ_E · (E_sun_cum + E_heat_cum + ε)
   901	```
   902	
   903	Denominator is the **cumulative throughput**, not the stored total — the correct reference for an open system whose meters grow unboundedly (this is why a raw "energy is constant" check is meaningless here). Two distinct precisions, stated honestly:
   904	
   905	- **Bookkeeping closure (each flux paired via §5.2 TwoSum):** closes to O(eps²). Catches any unpaired energy write (a mint). `τ_E_book = 1e-12`.
   906	- **Physics/work consistency (`E_chem` derived from mass; `E_KE`/`pWake`/`wDrag` from the f32 kernel):** limited to f32, `τ_E_phys = 1e-6` per step, `1e-4` drift over 1e6 steps, **gated on bounded-oscillating, not endpoint** (semi-implicit integrators oscillate — F6, §4.8). The f32-ness is a *fidelity* limit checked by the S0 oracle gate, not a conservation leak (the application is exact via TwoSum).
   907	
   908	#### 15.5 S1 / S3 energy-conservation test predicates (the deliverable Codex asked for)
   909	
   910	**S1** (abiotic + producer loop; reservoirs E1/E4/E5, no E2/E3):
   911	```
   912	S1-ENERGY-1 (bookkeeping):  | E_chem(t) − E_chem(0) − (E_sun_cum − E_heat_cum) | / (E_sun_cum+E_heat_cum+ε) < 1e-12   [f64, all fluxes TwoSum-paired]
   913	S1-ENERGY-2 (derived-not-stored):  AST/audit — E_chem is ONLY ever computed as e_N·(Bp+Bd+Bm); no independent E write to a producer pool exists (kills the sync-glue P1 anti-pattern). ⇒ mass closure (#5, 1e-12) IMPLIES energy closure for producer pools.
   914	S1-ENERGY-3 (crash is energetic):  during the §7 bloom-crash, the ΔE_chem<0 of the decline is matched cell-for-cell by E_heat_cum rise (respiration F-P/F-R) + E_chem(Bd) rise (mortality) — energy released/moved, never lost.
   915	```
   916	
   917	**S3** (full economy; all six reservoirs):
   918	```
   919	S3-ENERGY-1 (bookkeeping):  INV-ENERGY (§15.4) < 1e-9 rel-to-throughput  [mix of f64 mass-derived + f32 KE terms booked via TwoSum]
   920	S3-ENERGY-2 (metabolic identity):  per creature/step, ΔE_reserve == −(P_basal + p_in/η)·dt to 1e-6 f32; NO path banks mechanical work at implicit η=1 (design §4.8, plan:534/657).
   921	S3-ENERGY-3 (work consistency, inherited from S0):  |ΔE_KE − (p_in − (pWake+pFin+wDrag))·dt| / throughput < 1e-6 f32, bounded-oscillating over 1e5 steps.
   922	S3-ENERGY-4 (feeding closure):  e_N·I_bio == AE·e_N·I_bio (→reserve) + (1−AE)·e_N·I_bio (→Bd); INV-TRANSFER on the mass split < 1e-14 f64.
   923	S3-ENERGY-5 (no mint):  E_sun_cum is the ONLY reservoir permitted to rise without an internal debit; every other energy increase is a §5.2 paired transfer (audit test forbids raw E_i += x). ⇒ the population "sustains without a mint/cap knob" (plan:558/572) becomes a checkable predicate, not a claim.
   924	```

   196	## 3. Empty / zero-mass body finiteness (completes Codex #9; closes NP-2)
   197	
   198	**Closes:** Codex **#9** finiteness half (a zero-mass empty body still entered the solver and produced `1/0` then `0·∞`), blocker **B3** (explicit finite handling for empty/zero-mass bodies), and new-problem **NP-2** (`M00=M02=M22=0 ⇒ tr=0 ⇒ inv=∞ ⇒ NaN`). Strengthens Law 1 (fidelity — a bodyless creature has zero capability, not a NaN).
   199	
   200	**Decision.** The solve guards the determinant with a **scale-relative** degeneracy test (never the donor's absolute `1e-12`, `SwimEval.cs:1157`) and **masks the result to zero** for invalid/empty bodies, so no division by zero and no `0·∞` can occur:
   201	
   202	```python
   203	body_valid = alive & (masked_segment_sum(seg_mass, seg_mask) > 0)   # [W,N_cap] bool
   204	# 2x2 constrained system (resolution 7): M = [[M00,M02],[M02,M22]], P=(Px,Pz)
   205	tr    = M00 + M22                                    # natural scale
   206	det   = M00*M22 - M02*M02
   207	scale = tr*tr                                        # scale of det
   208	degenerate = (~body_valid) | (det.abs() <= EPS_REL*scale)     # EPS_REL ~ 1e-10 (f32-relative)
   209	det_safe   = torch.where(degenerate, torch.ones_like(det), det)
   210	dvx = ( M22*Px - M02*Pz) / det_safe
   211	dvz = (-M02*Px + M00*Pz) / det_safe
   212	dvx = torch.where(body_valid, dvx, torch.zeros_like(dvx))     # empty/invalid -> zero accel, finite
   213	dvz = torch.where(body_valid, dvz, torch.zeros_like(dvz))
   214	```
   215	
   216	For an empty body `M00=M02=M22=0` and `P=0` (no segments ⇒ `F_stream=0`): `det=0 → det_safe=1 → dv=0/1=0`, then masked to 0. **Output is finite (zero accel).** For a *valid but near-singular* body (slender/anisotropic), `degenerate` is triggered only by the scale-relative `det` test, and the regularized path of resolution 7 handles it *with the reaction impulse ledgered* — it is **not** silently floored here. The absolute `1e-12` floor (`SwimEval.cs:1157`) is retained **only** inside `solve_sym3_donor` for gain0 byte-conformance (resolution 7), never in production.
   217	
   218	**Test (`test_solve.py::empty_zero_mass_finite`):** a batch containing (i) an empty body, (ii) a single zero-mass sentinel-only body, (iii) a `MinX/Y/Z`-clamped `0.12×0.12×0.3` degenerate body (`SwimEval.cs:75`), (iv) a valid body — assert all outputs finite; empty/zero-mass rows exactly `0`; valid row matches oracle rel `<1e-4`; no `inf`/`nan` anywhere (`torch.isfinite(dv).all()`).
   219	
   220	---
   423	## 7. Regularization ledgered (repairs Codex #2 + #12; closes NP-3)
   424	
   425	**Closes:** Codex **#2** (the production 2×2 solver added *unledgered* Tikhonov mass, so once regularization fires `MΔv=P+J_c` is false), Codex **#12** (the "robust" solver used cancellation-prone `λ_min=(tr−disc)/2`, had no `tr=0` branch, floored bad determinants silently, changed dynamics via unaccounted regularization), blocker **B4** (regularization reflected in momentum + energy), and new-problem **NP-3** (regularization invalidates the KKT mechanics + energy gate). Strengthens Law 2 (books close) and Law 1 (fidelity — the gate uses the *true* physics, not a regularized surrogate).
   426	
   427	### 7.1 Conservation gates use the EXACT (unregularized) constrained solve
   428	
   429	The 2×2 constrained KKT solve (Rev-2 §2, unchanged in form) is the production dynamics:
   430	```
   431	[ M00 M02 ][dvx]   [Px]
   432	[ M02 M22 ][dvz] = [Pz]      P = F_stream·Dt,   dv_y = 0
   433	J_y = M01·dvx + M12·dvz − Py   (vertical reaction impulse, workless; ledger only)
   434	```
   435	**For every conservation gate (INV-CONSERVE, INV-ENERGY, `R_step`, momentum), the solve is the exact unregularized system.** Regularization is a numerical fallback that is *permitted only* for a valid-but-near-singular `M` — and empty/degenerate bodies are already excluded by the resolution-3 sentinel/`body_valid` mask, so the fallback fires only for genuinely ill-conditioned real bodies (10:1 slender, extreme anisotropy), never for empties.
   436	
   437	### 7.2 Numerically stable condition estimate (replaces `λ_min=(tr−disc)/2`; handles `tr=0`)
   438	
   439	The cancellation-prone `λ_min = (tr − disc)/2` (Rev-2 §12) is replaced by a cancellation-free form. For the symmetric 2×2 `[[M00,M02],[M02,M22]]` (SPD by construction: `M_body·I + Σ SPD added-mass`):
   440	```
   441	tr    = M00 + M22
   442	det   = M00*M22 − M02*M02
   443	disc  = sqrt( (M00 − M22)^2 + 4*M02^2 )     # NO cancellation: sum of squares, always >= 0
   444	lam_max = 0.5*(tr + disc)                    # well-conditioned (sum of positives)
   445	lam_min = det / max(lam_max, TINY)           # EXACT (lam_min*lam_max = det); avoids (tr−disc) cancellation
   446	kappa   = lam_max / max(lam_min, eps_rel*tr) # condition number
   447	```
   448	`disc = sqrt((M00−M22)² + 4M02²)` never subtracts large near-equal quantities (the donor/Rev-2 `tr²−4det` form does). `lam_min = det/lam_max` is exact and stable. **`tr=0` is not a special case here** — it can only occur for an invalid/empty body, which resolution 3 masks out *before* this code (`body_valid=False ⇒ result forced to 0`); a valid SPD body has `tr>0`. The `TINY`/`eps_rel·tr` guards prevent `0/0` only on the already-masked degenerate path.
   449	
   450	### 7.3 When regularization fires, its impulse and work are ledgered (closes #2, NP-3, B4)
   451	
   452	```
   453	reg    = where(body_valid & (kappa > KAPPA_MAX), EPS_SPD*tr, 0.0)   # KAPPA_MAX = 1e6 (f32)
   454	Mr00,Mr22 = M00+reg, M22+reg
   455	dv_reg = solve_2x2(Mr00, M02, Mr22, Px, Pz)                          # regularized dynamics (numerical fallback)
   456	# the regularization changed the momentum balance by:
   457	J_reg  = [ (Mr00-M00)*dvx_reg + 0,  (Mr22-M22)*dvz_reg ]  = reg * dv_reg   # reaction impulse from Tikhonov mass
   458	```
   459	`J_reg` is recorded so **momentum still closes**: `M·dv = P + J_c + J_reg` is now *true* (the added `reg·dv` is booked, not hidden). Its **work** `v_mid·J_reg` enters the numerical-residual **energy** ledger (resolution 1, int64 quanta), so `R_step` (resolution 6.2) closes with `J_reg` present. Regularization is thus a *ledgered numerical event*, not an invisible dynamics change. On the overwhelmingly-common well-conditioned path `reg=0`, `J_reg=0`, and production == exact solve bit-for-bit.
   460	
   461	**Regularization is never permitted in the gain0 donor-conformance path:** `solve_sym3_donor` (Rev-2 §12, reproducing `SwimEval.cs:1151-1166` byte-for-byte incl. the absolute `1e-12` floor) is used *only* to match untouched-donor gain0 fixtures; it never regularizes. Production `solve_constrained_xz` regularizes-and-ledgers. `test_solve.py::donor_conformance` asserts they agree on well-conditioned bodies.
   462	
   463	### 7.4 Tests
   464	
   465	`test_reg_momentum_closes` (force `κ>KAPPA_MAX` with a 10:1 slender body; assert `M·dv == P + J_c + J_reg` to f32 rel `<1e-5`, and that dropping `J_reg` makes it fail — proving the ledger is load-bearing). `test_reg_energy_ledgered` (assert `R_step` closes with `v_mid·J_reg` booked, fails without it). `test_stable_kappa_no_cancellation` (extreme-anisotropy corpus, Rev-2 §12: assert `disc` and `lam_min=det/lam_max` match a f64 eigen-reference to f32 rel `<1e-5`, whereas `(tr−disc)/2` diverges — proving the stable form is load-bearing). `test_reg_off_on_wellcond` (isotropic/H0: `reg==0`, production == exact solve, `max_abs(Δ)==0`).
   466	
   467	---

# Round 4 verdict: NOT APPROVABLE yet

Rev‑4 fixes the headline regularization sign, but the layered Rev‑2+Rev‑3+Rev‑4 spec still fails internal consistency. The largest problem is that D2’s int64 ledger is an exactly balanced expenditure ledger, not conservation of the simulator’s total physical energy.

Aliases below: `rev4` = `2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev4-corrections.md`, `rev3` = `2026-07-12-sirrobin-plan-rev3-reconciliation.md`, `rev2` = `2026-07-11-sirrobin-plan-rev2-corrections.md`, `v1` = `2026-07-11-sirrobin-implementation-plan.md`.

## 1. Seven required changes

1. **PARTIAL — regularization/solver.** The sign is now correct (`J_reg=-rΔv`), but the dispatch predicate is inconsistent with retained `κ>KAPPA_MAX`, both branch solves are eagerly evaluated without specified safe denominators, and the 2-D equation incorrectly adds the vertical `J_c` (`rev4:41-47,53-75`; `rev3:431-446,453-459`).

2. **STILL-BROKEN — energy representation and mechanisms.** Floats are removed from the new int64 equality, but the ledger excludes mechanical and biomass chemical energy while still claiming energy conservation; the overflow check is insufficient, and source/death transactions are not coherent (`rev4:154-176,231-248,289-328`).

3. **STILL-BROKEN — feeding/reserve replenishment.** Predation between two creatures can transfer reserve, but grazing `Bp` has no reserve to debit after biomass chemical energy is deleted; therefore the intended field-biomass→consumer-reserve path remains absent (`rev4:180-193`; `rev2:862-868`).

4. **PARTIAL — RNG continuation.** Dedicated attempt bits exist, but `gene_iid+draw_index→element` packing is unspecified, the Box–Muller expression has a precedence bug, and the probability inequality is wrong at the stated lower bound (`rev4:370-403,407-417`).

5. **STILL-BROKEN — gain1 fixtures.** Rev‑4 promises that literal inputs and expected values will be committed, but supplies neither; it remains a T11 deliverable rather than a frozen oracle specification (`rev4:445-468`).

6. **RESOLVED — #17 residuals.** The seven mutation symbols are named and the preserved bounds are correctly changed from `S_max` to `N_max/E_max` (`rev4:480-495`).

7. **PARTIAL — feeding stub/throughput.** All-OOM now correctly fails, but the byte model omits at least the `neigh_idx` and `dist2` reads, does not specify `dist2`’s tensor/dtype, and leaves `K` unpinned (`rev4:507-529`).

## 2. D2 two-currency architecture

### (a) Does the energy ledger close per step?

It closes arithmetically because every reserve debit is paired with an equal heat credit. That is tautological transfer conservation, not total physical-energy conservation.

For a step with no external source:

```text
Δreserve = −(P_basal + p_in/η)dt
Δheat    = +(P_basal + p_in/η)dt
```

Thus the int64 sum is exactly unchanged. But physical energy also contains `ΔKE`. During acceleration:

```text
Δ(reserve + heat + KE) = ΔKE ≠ 0
```

Rev‑4 avoids seeing this mismatch by excluding KE from the equality (`rev4:231-248`). Calling that “never an int64 leak” is category switching: the quantity is omitted, not conserved.

The gait-cycle argument is conditional, not a general conservation proof. It works only if:

- the cycle is truly periodic, so `∮ΔKE=0`;
- all mechanical work is eventually dissipated locally;
- no net acceleration, mechanical export, or non-periodic transient occurs.

S0 and later locomotion do not guarantee those conditions. Therefore there is a real per-step physical gap, even though the reduced metabolic books balance.

### (b) Is all muscle spend correctly booked as heat?

No. Immediate heat should be:

```text
P_basal + (1/η − 1)p_in
```

The remaining `p_in` is mechanical work delivered to the body/fluid. That was the correct Rev‑2 partition (`rev2:870-881`).

Booking the full `p_in/η` as heat anticipates eventual dissipation while mechanical work simultaneously exists in the f32 arm (`rev4:237-248`). It therefore overstates actual heat during transients. If `E_heat_cum` is renamed “irreversible metabolic expenditure,” the integer equation is valid—but it is not an energy/heat conservation ledger.

### (c) Do floats still enter an int64 equality?

The new canonical Rev‑4 equation itself does not: it contains only reserve and cumulative meters (`rev4:166-176,333-339`).

However, the layered spec still retains predicates involving float `E_chem` and `E_KE`, including the Rev‑2 S1/S3 energy gates (`rev2:899-923`). Rev‑4 explicitly deletes Rev‑3 §6.4, but does not cleanly replace every retained S1 flux/test using biomass chemical energy. The final spec therefore still contains incompatible energy definitions.

### (d) Are all reservoirs enumerated?

- **Mass: no.** The original closed inventory includes `Bm`, and S1 remineralization creates it (`v1:408,414,443-445`). Rev‑4’s claimed exhaustive mass equation omits `Bm` (`rev4:333-335`).
- **Energy: complete only for the deliberately narrow metabolic accounting set.** `{reserve,E_sun,E_heat,E_export}` is exhaustive for that definition, but not for physical energy because biomass chemical energy and KE are excluded.

There are additional transaction defects:

- `E_sun_cum` is a cumulative meter, not a source reservoir. Writing `E_sun_cum → reserve` as a transfer has the wrong semantics; external input must credit both reserve and the source meter (`rev4:159,166,182-184`).
- Death’s `reserve → E_heat / E_sun-return` is ambiguous and conflicts with `E_sun_cum` being a cumulative external-input meter (`rev4:304-312`).
- The supplied `transfer_quanta` is elementwise, but examples transfer `[W,N_cap]` reserve into `[W]` heat meters without specifying an exact grouped reduction (`rev4:199-206,293-297`).
- Growth’s two ledgers can cap independently: mass can grow after the energy debit is short, or energy can be spent after the mass source is short. The transaction is not atomic (`rev4:195-212,289-302`).
- Checking each value against `2^62` does not prevent the multi-term int64 sum from overflowing before the assertion. A checked aggregate absolute bound or wider accumulator is required (`rev4:318-328`).

## 3. Internal-consistency audit

**FAIL. Surviving contradictions:**

- **Gate (b) still has two meanings.** D2.5 declares gate (b) to be the force-law algebraic identity, then repeatedly calls `R_step` gate (b) (`rev4:220-248,255-282`). Rev‑3 says the S0 KE gate is `R_step` (`rev3:370-382`).
- **2-D versus 3-D momentum.** `M` and `P` are explicitly 2-D x/z tensors, yet the equation/test adds vertical `J_c` (`rev4:46-51,93-102`). The vertical reaction belongs only in a separate full-3-D statement.
- **Degeneracy rules disagree.** Rev‑4 uses `det<det_floor`/`λ_min<λ_floor` and calls that equivalent to `κ>KAPPA_MAX`; those are not generally equivalent. Retained Rev‑3 computes `reg` from `κ>KAPPA_MAX` (`rev4:54-68,71-73`; `rev3:441-453`).
- **Invalid execution is not safe as claimed.** `torch.where` does not short-circuit. Rev‑4 computes both singular `solve_2x2` branches before selecting INVALID, contrary to its “before any division/no 1/0” claim (`rev4:61-78`).
- **S1 biomass-energy processes have no D2 representation.** Retained production, respiration, remineralization, and burial move energy through `E_chem(Bp/Bd/Bm)` (`rev2:862-893`), while Rev‑4 declares that energy diagnostic-only (`rev4:141-150`).
- **Mass inventory omits `Bm`.** `rev4:333-335` conflicts with `v1:408,414,443-445`.

## 4. New Rev‑4 problems

- Box–Muller’s expression needs `(((w0 >> 8) & 0xFFFFFF) + 1)`. As written, Python parses the addition before bitwise `&` (`rev4:407-409`).
- For `p_accept=0.5`, failure is exactly `2^-256`, not `<2^-256` (`rev4:399-403`).
- `max(p_in,ε)` is unsafe normalization when signed reactive power makes `p_in<0`; use `max(abs(p_in),ε)` (`rev4:222-224`).
- The gain1 quadrature mapping omits the `1/t²` Jacobian from `λ=(1−t)/t` (`rev4:450-455`).
- The feeding-stub “exact” byte count is incomplete (`rev4:507-520`).

## 5. Phase impact

**Real S0/SpikeSwim blockers:**

- Unify gate (b): retain both algebraic force-law tests and the distinct `R_step` discrete-KE gate.
- Make the three-way solver executable: one canonical `κ` predicate, safe denominators for eagerly evaluated branches, and dimensionally correct x/z versus vertical momentum equations.
- Actually freeze gain1 fixture inputs and expected outputs.
- Correct the gain1 quadrature mapping/Jacobian.
- Preserve the non-OOM S0 authorization requirement.

**Later-phase blockers:**

- D2’s biomass/reserve/heat architecture, missing `Bm`, grazing reserve source, atomic growth caps, death semantics, grouped energy transfers, and overflow proof gate S1/S3—not starting the standalone locomotion kernel.
- RNG mutation details primarily gate S3, although the Box–Muller/parser defect should be fixed when the RNG scaffold lands.
- The feeding stub gates provisional G‑E2E at S2→S3, not the S0 locomotion benchmark.

S0 has no ecology, genome mutation, or steering (`v1:277`), and scaffold conservation runs on a fake reservoir (`v1:25`). D2 therefore should not block writing unrelated S0 code—but the live S0 solver, gate, and oracle contradictions do.

## 6. Minimal changes required before approval

1. Publish executable solver pseudocode using exactly `REGULARIZED = body_valid & (κ>KAPPA_MAX)`, safe branch denominators, and separate 2-D `MΔv=P+J_reg` from the full 3-D vertical-reaction statement.
2. State that gate (b) is `R_step`; retain the force-law identities as separate algebraic subtests.
3. Commit literal H1 fixture inputs and expected values, including the correct transformed quadrature with Jacobian.
4. Reframe D2 as a metabolic-expenditure ledger, or restore a physically complete hybrid energy balance. Do not call the current reduced sum total energy conservation.
5. Before S1/S3, restore `Bm`, define energy-bearing biomass/reserve feeding, make cross-ledger transactions atomic under source caps, define grouped int64 credits, and provide an aggregate overflow-safe summation rule.

Rev‑4 is close enough to justify continuing preparatory scaffold work, but not yet internally coherent enough to authorize the SpikeSwim implementation plan as written.

3. **STILL-BROKEN — feeding/reserve replenishment.** Predation between two creatures can transfer reserve, but grazing `Bp` has no reserve to debit after biomass chemical energy is deleted; therefore the intended field-biomass→consumer-reserve path remains absent (`rev4:180-193`; `rev2:862-868`).

4. **PARTIAL — RNG continuation.** Dedicated attempt bits exist, but `gene_iid+draw_index→element` packing is unspecified, the Box–Muller expression has a precedence bug, and the probability inequality is wrong at the stated lower bound (`rev4:370-403,407-417`).

5. **STILL-BROKEN — gain1 fixtures.** Rev‑4 promises that literal inputs and expected values will be committed, but supplies neither; it remains a T11 deliverable rather than a frozen oracle specification (`rev4:445-468`).

6. **RESOLVED — #17 residuals.** The seven mutation symbols are named and the preserved bounds are correctly changed from `S_max` to `N_max/E_max` (`rev4:480-495`).

7. **PARTIAL — feeding stub/throughput.** All-OOM now correctly fails, but the byte model omits at least the `neigh_idx` and `dist2` reads, does not specify `dist2`’s tensor/dtype, and leaves `K` unpinned (`rev4:507-529`).

## 2. D2 two-currency architecture

### (a) Does the energy ledger close per step?

It closes arithmetically because every reserve debit is paired with an equal heat credit. That is tautological transfer conservation, not total physical-energy conservation.

For a step with no external source:

```text
Δreserve = −(P_basal + p_in/η)dt
Δheat    = +(P_basal + p_in/η)dt
```

Thus the int64 sum is exactly unchanged. But physical energy also contains `ΔKE`. During acceleration:

```text
Δ(reserve + heat + KE) = ΔKE ≠ 0
```

Rev‑4 avoids seeing this mismatch by excluding KE from the equality (`rev4:231-248`). Calling that “never an int64 leak” is category switching: the quantity is omitted, not conserved.

The gait-cycle argument is conditional, not a general conservation proof. It works only if:

- the cycle is truly periodic, so `∮ΔKE=0`;
- all mechanical work is eventually dissipated locally;
- no net acceleration, mechanical export, or non-periodic transient occurs.

S0 and later locomotion do not guarantee those conditions. Therefore there is a real per-step physical gap, even though the reduced metabolic books balance.

### (b) Is all muscle spend correctly booked as heat?

No. Immediate heat should be:

```text
P_basal + (1/η − 1)p_in
```

The remaining `p_in` is mechanical work delivered to the body/fluid. That was the correct Rev‑2 partition (`rev2:870-881`).

Booking the full `p_in/η` as heat anticipates eventual dissipation while mechanical work simultaneously exists in the f32 arm (`rev4:237-248`). It therefore overstates actual heat during transients. If `E_heat_cum` is renamed “irreversible metabolic expenditure,” the integer equation is valid—but it is not an energy/heat conservation ledger.

### (c) Do floats still enter an int64 equality?

The new canonical Rev‑4 equation itself does not: it contains only reserve and cumulative meters (`rev4:166-176,333-339`).

However, the layered spec still retains predicates involving float `E_chem` and `E_KE`, including the Rev‑2 S1/S3 energy gates (`rev2:899-923`). Rev‑4 explicitly deletes Rev‑3 §6.4, but does not cleanly replace every retained S1 flux/test using biomass chemical energy. The final spec therefore still contains incompatible energy definitions.

### (d) Are all reservoirs enumerated?

- **Mass: no.** The original closed inventory includes `Bm`, and S1 remineralization creates it (`v1:408,414,443-445`). Rev‑4’s claimed exhaustive mass equation omits `Bm` (`rev4:333-335`).
- **Energy: complete only for the deliberately narrow metabolic accounting set.** `{reserve,E_sun,E_heat,E_export}` is exhaustive for that definition, but not for physical energy because biomass chemical energy and KE are excluded.

There are additional transaction defects:

- `E_sun_cum` is a cumulative meter, not a source reservoir. Writing `E_sun_cum → reserve` as a transfer has the wrong semantics; external input must credit both reserve and the source meter (`rev4:159,166,182-184`).
- Death’s `reserve → E_heat / E_sun-return` is ambiguous and conflicts with `E_sun_cum` being a cumulative external-input meter (`rev4:304-312`).
- The supplied `transfer_quanta` is elementwise, but examples transfer `[W,N_cap]` reserve into `[W]` heat meters without specifying an exact grouped reduction (`rev4:199-206,293-297`).
- Growth’s two ledgers can cap independently: mass can grow after the energy debit is short, or energy can be spent after the mass source is short. The transaction is not atomic (`rev4:195-212,289-302`).
- Checking each value against `2^62` does not prevent the multi-term int64 sum from overflowing before the assertion. A checked aggregate absolute bound or wider accumulator is required (`rev4:318-328`).

## 3. Internal-consistency audit

**FAIL. Surviving contradictions:**

- **Gate (b) still has two meanings.** D2.5 declares gate (b) to be the force-law algebraic identity, then repeatedly calls `R_step` gate (b) (`rev4:220-248,255-282`). Rev‑3 says the S0 KE gate is `R_step` (`rev3:370-382`).
- **2-D versus 3-D momentum.** `M` and `P` are explicitly 2-D x/z tensors, yet the equation/test adds vertical `J_c` (`rev4:46-51,93-102`). The vertical reaction belongs only in a separate full-3-D statement.
- **Degeneracy rules disagree.** Rev‑4 uses `det<det_floor`/`λ_min<λ_floor` and calls that equivalent to `κ>KAPPA_MAX`; those are not generally equivalent. Retained Rev‑3 computes `reg` from `κ>KAPPA_MAX` (`rev4:54-68,71-73`; `rev3:441-453`).
- **Invalid execution is not safe as claimed.** `torch.where` does not short-circuit. Rev‑4 computes both singular `solve_2x2` branches before selecting INVALID, contrary to its “before any division/no 1/0” claim (`rev4:61-78`).
- **S1 biomass-energy processes have no D2 representation.** Retained production, respiration, remineralization, and burial move energy through `E_chem(Bp/Bd/Bm)` (`rev2:862-893`), while Rev‑4 declares that energy diagnostic-only (`rev4:141-150`).
- **Mass inventory omits `Bm`.** `rev4:333-335` conflicts with `v1:408,414,443-445`.

## 4. New Rev‑4 problems

- Box–Muller’s expression needs `(((w0 >> 8) & 0xFFFFFF) + 1)`. As written, Python parses the addition before bitwise `&` (`rev4:407-409`).
- For `p_accept=0.5`, failure is exactly `2^-256`, not `<2^-256` (`rev4:399-403`).
- `max(p_in,ε)` is unsafe normalization when signed reactive power makes `p_in<0`; use `max(abs(p_in),ε)` (`rev4:222-224`).
- The gain1 quadrature mapping omits the `1/t²` Jacobian from `λ=(1−t)/t` (`rev4:450-455`).
- The feeding-stub “exact” byte count is incomplete (`rev4:507-520`).

## 5. Phase impact

**Real S0/SpikeSwim blockers:**

- Unify gate (b): retain both algebraic force-law tests and the distinct `R_step` discrete-KE gate.
- Make the three-way solver executable: one canonical `κ` predicate, safe denominators for eagerly evaluated branches, and dimensionally correct x/z versus vertical momentum equations.
- Actually freeze gain1 fixture inputs and expected outputs.
- Correct the gain1 quadrature mapping/Jacobian.
- Preserve the non-OOM S0 authorization requirement.

**Later-phase blockers:**

- D2’s biomass/reserve/heat architecture, missing `Bm`, grazing reserve source, atomic growth caps, death semantics, grouped energy transfers, and overflow proof gate S1/S3—not starting the standalone locomotion kernel.
- RNG mutation details primarily gate S3, although the Box–Muller/parser defect should be fixed when the RNG scaffold lands.
- The feeding stub gates provisional G‑E2E at S2→S3, not the S0 locomotion benchmark.

S0 has no ecology, genome mutation, or steering (`v1:277`), and scaffold conservation runs on a fake reservoir (`v1:25`). D2 therefore should not block writing unrelated S0 code—but the live S0 solver, gate, and oracle contradictions do.

## 6. Minimal changes required before approval

1. Publish executable solver pseudocode using exactly `REGULARIZED = body_valid & (κ>KAPPA_MAX)`, safe branch denominators, and separate 2-D `MΔv=P+J_reg` from the full 3-D vertical-reaction statement.
2. State that gate (b) is `R_step`; retain the force-law identities as separate algebraic subtests.
3. Commit literal H1 fixture inputs and expected values, including the correct transformed quadrature with Jacobian.
4. Reframe D2 as a metabolic-expenditure ledger, or restore a physically complete hybrid energy balance. Do not call the current reduced sum total energy conservation.
5. Before S1/S3, restore `Bm`, define energy-bearing biomass/reserve feeding, make cross-ledger transactions atomic under source caps, define grouped int64 credits, and provide an aggregate overflow-safe summation rule.

Rev‑4 is close enough to justify continuing preparatory scaffold work, but not yet internally coherent enough to authorize the SpikeSwim implementation plan as written.
